from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-516` | The code generation source (randomness requirement) is not stated. | A security policy |\n| `GAP-BE-517` | What happens when the email transport fails after the challenge and send attempt are stored is not stated, including whether the attempt still counts. | A failure-handling decision |\n| `GAP-BE-518` | Retention for locks (5 days, `ASM-BE-303`) could delete a non-zero `failed_count` that the no-window rule (`ASM-BE-305`) would otherwise keep. | A rule reconciling retention with the counting window |\n| `GAP-BE-519` | The success statuses and response bodies of both operations are not stated in any input. | A contract decision, rendered by DevAgent |\n| `GAP-BE-520` | No latency or availability target exists for either operation (see also `GAP-BE-301`). | A non-functional requirement |\n| `GAP-BE-521` | Whether the existing authentication shares the `users` table is not stated. | A description of the existing authentication (`GAP-BE-085`) |\n\n**Assumptions raised by this module.**\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-503` | A verify refused because of a lock changes neither `failed_count` nor `locked_until`. | BR-022 refuses verification during the lock and states no extension. | A rule from the product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-504` | A code is expired only when the instant is strictly after issue + 10 minutes. | BR-024 and US-006-06 say \"after\". BR-020 and BR-026 say \"expires\", so `GAP-BE-306` stays open. | A decision on `GAP-BE-306` |\n| `ASM-BE-505` | Column types: uuid keys, text email, timestamptz instants, integer counter, bigint identity. | PostgreSQL 16 is the platform and no types are supplied. | A platform decision |\n| `ASM-BE-506` | Only the latest unconsumed challenge for an email is checked at verification. | No input says whether earlier codes stay valid (`GAP-BE-083`). One live code is the narrower reading. | A rule from the product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-507` | A refused send writes no `send_attempts` row. | Otherwise a caller that keeps retrying is never released from the window. | A rule from the product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-508` | `failed_count` is reset to 0 when a lock is entered, so it ranges 0 to 4. | Otherwise the first wrong code after the lock would re-lock immediately. | A rule from the product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-509` | Only a wrong code against the latest unexpired, unconsumed challenge counts toward the lock. Expired and consumed codes do not. | BR-022 says \"wrong codes\". An expired or used code is not wrong, only refused. | A rule from the product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-510` | `login_locks` has an `updated_at` column used as the retention basis. | NFR-019 gives a period but no basis for locks. | A rule from the product owner |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-006-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/notes-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/notes-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: notes\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [CON-BE-003, Q-BE-003, CON-BE-300, Q-BE-300, Q-BE-012]\nscope: [API-NOTES-232390, API-NOTES-045877, API-NOTES-847551, API-NOTES-359413, API-NOTES-150096, API-NOTES-810640, API-NOTES-917869]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `notes` module owns the note lifecycle. [backend.module-map; stories.md EP-002]\n\n1. Create a note with a mandatory title and body. `pinned` defaults to false. Operation `API-NOTES-232390`. [stories.md US-002-01; requirements.md BR-005, BR-018]\n2. Read one note by id, including tags, archive status and the pinned flag. Operation `API-NOTES-045877`. [stories.md US-002-06]\n3. Partially edit a note, including pinning and unpinning through `pinned`, without overwriting any field that was not sent. Operation `API-NOTES-847551`. [stories.md US-002-02; requirements.md BR-006, BR-018]\n4. Add and remove tags. Adding an existing tag creates no duplicate. Operations `API-NOTES-359413` and `API-NOTES-150096`. [stories.md US-002-03]\n5. Archive and unarchive a note. Archiving is a status change that never deletes data and is fully reversible. Operations `API-NOTES-810640` and `API-NOTES-917869`. [stories.md US-002-04, US-002-05; requirements.md BR-007, BR-008]\n6. Own the NOTE, TAG and NOTE_TAG data. [backend.module-map]\n7. Own the NFR-019 retention deletion of notes. That part is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-300` and `Q-BE-300` (BE-NOTES-FR-021). [backend.module-map; requirements.md NFR-019]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Keyword and tag search, pagination, the archived filter and pinned-first ordering. `search` owns them and only reads `notes` data. [backend.module-map; requirements.md BR-009 to BR-011, BR-019]\n- Sign-in, the credential, and user creation. `auth` owns them (`Q-BE-012`).\n- The note access rule and note ownership. They are `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-003` and `Q-BE-003`.\n- The OpenAPI document, example payloads and the global error envelope. `api-docs` and `backend.error-model` own them.\n- Rejection of attachment, real-time and mobile-specific calls. `scope-guardrail` owns it.\n- Screens for listing, editing, tagging and archiving. The web-app surface owns them. [stories.md US-006-01 to US-006-05]\n- Any note-deletion operation. None is in scope (`ASM-BE-565`).\n\n**Operations owned.** Seven, referenced by id only: `API-NOTES-232390`, `API-NOTES-045877`, `API-NOTES-847551`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096`, `API-NOTES-810640`, `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-553` onward, `GAP-BE-564` onward, `INV-BE-062` onward, `Q-BE-351` onward. It mints no `CON-` item. All other ids are cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\nNo input states column types, lengths or constraints. The platform is PostgreSQL 16. [platform.md] The types below are a proposal (`ASM-BE-553`). Entity names follow `backend.domain-model`.\n\n**notes**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | server-generated | primary key; never client-supplied (`ASM-BE-555`) |\n| `title` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (length(btrim(title)) > 0)` (`ASM-BE-556`) |\n| `body` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (length(btrim(body)) > 0)` (`ASM-BE-556`) |\n| `status` | text | NOT NULL | `'active'` | `CHECK (status IN ('active','archived'))` (`ASM-BE-554`) |\n| `pinned` | boolean | NOT NULL | `false` | BR-018 gives a default and no null state (`ASM-BE-034`) |\n| `created_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | set at insert | never updated after insert |\n| `updated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | set at insert | `CHECK (updated_at >= created_at)` |\n\nNo `owner` column is defined. It is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-003` and `Q-BE-003`, because no input relates a note to a user (`GAP-BE-003`). Maximum lengths of `title` and `body` are not stated (`GAP-BE-035`), so none are set.\n\n**tags**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `name` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key; compared exactly (`ASM-BE-567`) |\n\nName format, length and case rules are `GAP-BE-035` and `GAP-BE-571`.\n\n**note_tags**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `note_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | FK \u2192 `notes(id)` `ON DELETE CASCADE` (`ASM-BE-566`) |\n| `tag_name` | text | NOT NULL | none | FK \u2192 `tags(name)` `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n\nPrimary key `(note_id, tag_name)`. This key is what prevents a duplicate tag entry on a note. [stories.md US-002-03]\n\n**Indexes.**\n\n| Index | Query it serves |\n|---|---|\n| primary key on `notes(id)` | get, patch, tag, archive and unarchive by id |\n| primary key on `note_tags(note_id, tag_name)` | list the tags of one note; duplicate prevention |\n| btree on `note_tags(tag_name, note_id)` | notes carrying a given tag, read by `search` for tag filters |\n\nIndexes serving keyword search and the 300 ms SLA belong to `search` (migration 003 in `backend.migration-plan`). They are not defined here.\n\n**Time.** Every instant is `timestamptz`, stored as a UTC instant (`ASM-BE-553`).\n\n**Encryption and PII.** Note content may be sensitive. No input states encryption at rest, so none is claimed (`GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-033`). No note field is declared PII by any input. The handling policy is `GAP-BE-017`.\n\n**Migrations.** `notes`, `tags` and `note_tags` are created by migration 001 `notes-core` in `backend.migration-plan`. This module adds no migration. The retention deletion of notes is migration 006 and is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-300`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nThe only status column is `notes.status`. States: `active` (initial, `ASM-BE-554`) and `archived`. **Neither state is terminal**, because archiving must always be reversible. [requirements.md BR-008]\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `active` | `API-NOTES-232390` | title and body valid | row inserted; `pinned` as supplied or false |\n| `active` | `archived` | `API-NOTES-810640` | note exists | `status` and `updated_at` change; nothing else |\n| `archived` | `active` | `API-NOTES-917869` | note exists | `status` and `updated_at` change; all original data preserved |\n| `archived` | `archived` | `API-NOTES-810640` | | refused as conflict (`ALREADY_ARCHIVED`, status `GAP-BE-309`); no data modified |\n| `active` | `active` | `API-NOTES-917869` | | refused as the same conflict (`ASM-BE-559`); no data modified |\n| `active` or `archived` | same | `API-NOTES-847551`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096` | note exists | status unchanged. Behaviour on an archived note is `ASM-BE-558`. |\n\n**Illegal transitions.** Archive of an archived note returns the conflict response. [stories.md US-002-04] Unarchive of an active note follows `ASM-BE-559`. The codes are in `backend.error-model`.\n\n**Deletion.** The inputs describe one deletion: the NFR-019 retention deletion of notes. It would add a terminal state to a lifecycle that BR-007, BR-008 and NFR-005 define as non-destructive. It is therefore not defined here and is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-300` and `Q-BE-300`.\n\n**Badge derivation.** The archived/active indicator is a function of `status` alone. No other field participates. [stories.md US-006-01 shows an indicator and no input derives it from anything else]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-001 Create a note\n**Given** a valid title and body,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** the note is persisted with a unique server-assigned id, `created_at` and `updated_at` set, `status` `active`, and `pinned` false, and the response is 201 with the created note.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-01; BR-005, BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-002 Create a pinned note\n**Given** a valid payload with `pinned` true,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** the response is 201 and the stored note has `pinned` true.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-02; BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-pinned` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-003 Reject an invalid create\n**Given** a payload with a missing, null or empty title or body (whitespace-only counts as empty, `ASM-BE-556`),\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** the response is 400 with `VALIDATION_ERROR` and no note is persisted.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-03; BR-005.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-missing-title`, `test-notes-create-missing-body`, `test-notes-create-whitespace-only` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`. Field limits are `GAP-BE-035`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-004 Read a note\n**Given** an existing note id,\n**When** `API-NOTES-045877` is called,\n**Then** the response is 200 with full note details including tags, archive status and the pinned flag.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-get-existing` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-045877`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-005 Read an unknown note\n**Given** an id that was never assigned to a note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-045877` is called,\n**Then** the response is 404 `NOT_FOUND` and no partial data is exposed. \"Deleted\" in the source story is treated as never-existing (`ASM-BE-565`). A malformed id is `GAP-BE-572`.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-get-unknown-404` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-045877`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-006 Partial edit\n**Given** an existing note and valid updated fields,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** the response is 200 with the updated note, `updated_at` is set to the time of the change (`ASM-BE-562`), and every field absent from the request is unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-01; BR-006.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-partial-preserves-fields` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`. Whether the body may carry tags is `GAP-BE-564`. PUT versus PATCH is `GAP-BE-309`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-007 Pin and unpin\n**Given** a PATCH containing only `pinned`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** the response is 200, `pinned` is updated, and title, body and tags are unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-02; BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-pinned-only` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-008 Edit an unknown note\n**Given** a non-existent note id,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** the response is 404 `NOT_FOUND` and no data is modified.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-unknown-404` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-009 Reject an invalid edit\n**Given** an existing note and a PATCH that sets title or body to null, empty or whitespace-only (`ASM-BE-557`), or sets `pinned` to a non-boolean,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** the response is 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` and the note is unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** BR-005 (extended to edits by `ASM-BE-557`); US-002-02; NFR-005. Not cited as an edit rule in any input.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-empty-title-rejected`, `test-notes-patch-invalid-pinned` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-010 Add a tag\n**Given** an existing note and a tag name,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called,\n**Then** the response is 200 and the note's tag list includes the tag.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`. Name rules are `GAP-BE-035` and `GAP-BE-571`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-011 Add an existing tag\n**Given** a note that already carries the tag,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called with the same name,\n**Then** the response is 200, no duplicate entry is created, and `updated_at` is unchanged (`ASM-BE-562`).\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03; AC for the same story.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add-idempotent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-012 Remove a tag\n**Given** an existing note that carries the tag,\n**When** `API-NOTES-150096` is called,\n**Then** the tag is no longer on the note, and title, body, pinned and status are unchanged.\n- **Given** the tag is not on the note, **Then** the response is 404 `NOT_FOUND` (`ASM-BE-560`, `Q-BE-353`).\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03; US-006-04 (consumer need). The success status and the absent-tag case are not stated in an input (`GAP-BE-565`, `GAP-BE-573`).\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-remove`, `test-notes-tag-remove-absent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-150096`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-013 Archive a note\n**Given** an active, existing note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** the response is 200, `status` becomes `archived`, and title, body, pinned, tags and `created_at` are intact.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-04; BR-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive-preserves-data` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-014 Archive an archived note\n**Given** an already-archived note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** a conflict response with `ALREADY_ARCHIVED` is returned and no data is modified. The status is `GAP-BE-309`.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive-twice-conflict` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-015 Unarchive a note\n**Given** an archived note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** the response is 200, `status` becomes `active`, and all original data is preserved.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-05; BR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive-restores-all-data`, `test-notes-archive-unarchive-roundtrip` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-016 Unarchive an unknown note\n**Given** a note id that does not exist,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** the response is 404 `NOT_FOUND`.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive-unknown-404` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-017 Unarchive an active note\n**Given** an active note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** the same conflict response as FR-014 is returned and no data is modified (`ASM-BE-559`, `Q-BE-352`).\n- **Traces to.** Not cited. Mirrors US-002-04's second criterion. Candidate for deletion if `Q-BE-352` rejects the default.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive-active-conflict` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-018 Unknown note on tag and archive operations\n**Given** a note id that does not exist,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096` or `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** the response is 404 `NOT_FOUND` and nothing is created or modified (`ASM-BE-563`).\n- **Traces to.** Pattern of US-002-02, US-002-05, US-002-06. Not cited for these three operations.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add-unknown-404`, `test-notes-tag-remove-unknown-404`, `test-notes-archive-unknown-404` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096`, `API-NOTES-810640`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-019 Input safety\n**Given** note title, body or tag fields containing injection payloads,\n**When** any operation of this module is called,\n**Then** the values are validated and handled as data only, and the security scan reports 0 critical injection vulnerabilities per release.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-injection-payloads-inert` (planned); the release security scan.\n- **API.** all seven operations.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-020 Concurrent edits\n**Given** two PATCH requests to one note at the same time,\n**When** both are processed,\n**Then** each request is applied atomically, so no request is applied half-way, with last-write-wins semantics and no broadcast or merge logic (`ASM-BE-561`, `Q-BE-354`).\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03 (second criterion); NFR-005.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-concurrent-atomic` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-021 Retention deletion of notes \u2014 `BLOCKED`\nNFR-019 read positionally says notes are deleted after 1 day (`ASM-BE-303`). That contradicts BR-007, BR-008 and NFR-005 (`CON-BE-300`). **Unblocked by** an answer to `Q-BE-300` and a corrected NFR-019. Until then no deletion is specified, built or scheduled.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-019; BR-007; BR-008; NFR-005.\n- **Verification.** none until unblocked.\n\n### Story coverage\n\n| Story | Requirements |\n|---|---|\n| US-002-01 | FR-001, FR-002, FR-003 |\n| US-002-02 | FR-006, FR-007, FR-008, FR-009, FR-020 |\n| US-002-03 | FR-010, FR-011, FR-012, FR-018 |\n| US-002-04 | FR-013, FR-014 |\n| US-002-05 | FR-015, FR-016, FR-017 |\n| US-002-06 | FR-004, FR-005 |\n\nAll six owned stories are covered. FR-019 and FR-021 trace to NFRs.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390` | POST | `/notes` | `notes` | 201 | 400, 401, 403, 500 | Create a note (US-002-01). Title and body mandatory and non-empty; pinned defaults to false. |\n| `API-NOTES-045877` | GET | `/notes/{id}` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404, 500 | Read a single note by id (US-002-06). Unknown id returns not found with no partial data. |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` | PATCH | `/notes/{id}` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404, 500 | Partially edit a note, including pinning/unpinning via pinned (US-002-02). Fields not sent are never overwritten. |\n| `API-NOTES-810640` | POST | `/notes/{id}/archive` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404, 409, 500 | Archive a note (US-002-04). Status change only; no data is deleted. Already-archived returns a conflict (status proposed, unstated in inputs). |\n| `API-NOTES-359413` | POST | `/notes/{id}/tags` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404, 500 | Add a tag to a note (US-002-03). Adding an existing tag creates no duplicate. |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` | DELETE | `/notes/{id}/tags/{name}` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404, 500 | Remove a tag from a note (US-002-03). |\n| `API-NOTES-917869` | POST | `/notes/{id}/unarchive` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404, 500 | Unarchive a note, restoring it to active with all data preserved (US-002-05). |\n\n### `API-NOTES-232390` \u2014 POST `/notes`\n\nCreate a note (US-002-01). Title and body mandatory and non-empty; pinned defaults to false.\n\n**Request:** \n- `title` (string, required): Note title, mandatory and non-empty.\n- `body` (string, required): Note body, mandatory and non-empty.\n- `pinned` (boolean): Whether the note is pinned; defaults to false.\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `title` (string, required): Note title.\n- `body` (string, required): Note body.\n- `status` (string, required): active or archived; new notes are active.\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag.\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note.\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-045877` \u2014 GET `/notes/{id}`\n\nRead a single note by id (US-002-06). Unknown id returns not found with no partial data.\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier.\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `title` (string, required): Note title.\n- `body` (string, required): Note body.\n- `status` (string, required): active or archived.\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag.\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note.\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-847551` \u2014 PATCH `/notes/{id}`\n\nPartially edit a note, including pinning/unpinning via pinned (US-002-02). Fields not sent are never overwritten.\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier.\n- `title` (string): New title; must be non-empty if sent.\n- `body` (string): New body; must be non-empty if sent.\n- `pinned` (boolean): Pin (true) or unpin (false) the note.\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `title` (string, required): Note title.\n- `body` (string, required): Note body.\n- `status` (string, required): active or archived.\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag.\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note.\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-810640` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/archive`\n\nArchive a note (US-002-04). Status change only; no data is deleted. Already-archived returns a conflict (status proposed, unstated in inputs).\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier.\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `status` (string, required): archived after success.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-359413` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/tags`\n\nAdd a tag to a note (US-002-03). Adding an existing tag creates no duplicate.\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier.\n- `name` (string, required): Tag name to add.\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `tags` (array, required): Resulting tag names on the note, without duplicates.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-150096` \u2014 DELETE `/notes/{id}/tags/{name}`\n\nRemove a tag from a note (US-002-03).\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier.\n- `name` (string, required): Path parameter: tag name to remove.\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `tags` (array, required): Resulting tag names on the note.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-917869` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/unarchive`\n\nUnarchive a note, restoring it to active with all data preserved (US-002-05).\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier.\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `status` (string, required): active after success.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\nProject-level invariants that apply to this module are cited, not restated: `INV-BE-008` (a PATCH never overwrites a field it does not contain) and `INV-BE-009` (archive and unarchive preserve all note data, threatened by `CON-BE-300`). `INV-BE-001` applies to list and search results and belongs to `search`.\n\n- `INV-BE-062`. After every operation, every note has a non-empty title, a non-empty body, a `status` of `active` or `archived`, and a non-null boolean `pinned`. [requirements.md BR-005, BR-018]\n- `INV-BE-063`. A note carries each tag at most once. [stories.md US-002-03]\n- `INV-BE-064`. A request that returns a 4xx response leaves the note, its tags and its `updated_at` unchanged. [stories.md AC-002-01-03, AC-002-02-03, US-002-04]\n- `INV-BE-065`. Archive and unarchive change only `status` and `updated_at`. Title, body, `pinned`, tags and `created_at` are unchanged. [requirements.md BR-007, BR-008]\n- `INV-BE-066`. `created_at` never changes after creation, and `updated_at >= created_at`.\n- `INV-BE-067`. A note id is assigned once by the server, is unique, and is never reused for another note (`ASM-BE-555`). [stories.md AC-002-01-01]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**Blocked.** The ownership predicate and the role permissions cannot be written until `Q-BE-003` and `CON-BE-003` are decided. No input relates a note to a user (`GAP-BE-003`) and BR-031, the note access rule, is corrupted as supplied. The credential that proves the caller is `Q-BE-012`. **Unblocked by** an answer to `Q-BE-003` and a corrected BR-031.\n\n**Provisional position, not a decision.** It is the `Q-BE-001` default recorded in `backend.auth-model`: any authenticated team member may call every operation of this module on any note.\n\n| Operation | Unauthenticated caller | Authenticated team member | Other actors |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390`, `API-NOTES-045877`, `API-NOTES-847551`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096`, `API-NOTES-810640`, `API-NOTES-917869` | Not stated, so denied (`ASM-BE-302`) | Provisional allow under `Q-BE-001`, `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-003` | Not stated, so denied (`ASM-BE-302`) |\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None exists. Ownership columns and the per-note access check are `BLOCKED`.\n\n**Dependency.** `notes` depends on `auth` for authentication. [backend.module-map] The unauthenticated and forbidden responses are `UNAUTHENTICATED` (401) and `FORBIDDEN` (403) in `backend.error-model`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n**Scheduled work.** The only candidate is the NFR-019 retention deletion of notes. It is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-300` and `Q-BE-300`. Its scheduler and frequency are `GAP-BE-101`. No sweep, retry or background job is specified for this module.\n\n**Retries.** None specified. No input states a retry policy.\n\n**Idempotency.**\n\n| Operation | Behaviour |\n|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-359413` | Idempotent by definition: adding an existing tag returns 200 and creates no duplicate (FR-011). [stories.md US-002-03] |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` | A repeat call on an absent tag follows `ASM-BE-560`. |\n| `API-NOTES-810640`, `API-NOTES-917869` | Not idempotent. A repeat returns the conflict response (FR-014, FR-017). |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` | Setting the same values twice has the same result. `updated_at` moves on each call (`ASM-BE-562`). |\n| `API-NOTES-232390` | No idempotency key is defined. A client retry after a lost response can create a duplicate note (`GAP-BE-569`). |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit.**\n- Validation of title and body: missing, null, empty, whitespace-only (FR-003, FR-009).\n- PATCH merge logic: only sent fields change, `pinned`-only case (FR-006, FR-007; `INV-BE-008`).\n- State guard for archive and unarchive (FR-013 to FR-017).\n- `updated_at` rules (`ASM-BE-562`), using an injectable clock.\n- Tag set semantics: no duplicate, absent-tag removal.\n\n**Integration.** Against PostgreSQL 16 (the platform, platform.md).\n- All FR tests named above.\n- Archive then unarchive round trip restores all data (`INV-BE-065`).\n- Every 4xx leaves the database unchanged (`INV-BE-064`).\n- Concurrent PATCH atomicity and concurrent tag add (FR-020, `INV-BE-063`).\n- Constraint tests: duplicate `note_tags` row, invalid `status`, null `pinned`.\n- Pinned notes created and listed are verified in `search`'s tests, not here.\n\n**Contract.**\n- Each of the seven operations is present in the generated OpenAPI document with request and response schemas and its 4xx/5xx responses. [requirements.md BR-012, NFR-008]\n- The error codes used by this module match `backend.error-model`.\n- The seven operations are covered by the endpoint-coverage check. [stories.md US-004-04]\n- No attachment, real-time or mobile-specific route is defined by this module. The guardrail suite itself belongs to `scope-guardrail`. [requirements.md NFR-010]\n\n**Security.** Injection payloads in title, body and tag name (FR-019). [requirements.md NFR-012]\n\n**Not testable until unblocked.** The access rule (`CON-BE-003`) and the retention deletion (`CON-BE-300`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions, assumptions and questions\n\n**Contradictions.** This module mints none. It depends on `CON-BE-003` (note access rule, Critical), `CON-BE-300` (retention deletion of notes, Critical) and `CON-BE-019` (differing change-request labels). Each is defined in the project spec.\n\n**Project-level items this module depends on.** `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-003`, `Q-BE-012`, `Q-BE-300`, `GAP-BE-003`, `GAP-BE-017`, `GAP-BE-033`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-089`, `GAP-BE-101`, `GAP-BE-300`, `GAP-BE-303`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-309`, `ASM-BE-034`, `ASM-BE-302`, `ASM-BE-303`.\n\n### Questions\n\nThis run is unattended. Each `degradable` question takes its stated default and is not asked.\n\n| ID | Class | Question | Default taken |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-351` | degradable | May an archived note be edited, pinned or tagged? | Yes, as an active note (`ASM-BE-558`) |\n| `Q-BE-352` | degradable | What does unarchive of an active note return? | The archive conflict (`ASM-BE-559`) |\n| `Q-BE-353` | degradable | What does removing a tag that is not on the note return? | 404 `NOT_FOUND` (`ASM-BE-560`) |\n| `Q-BE-354` | degradable | Is concurrent PATCH last-write-wins or a conflict? | Last-write-wins (`ASM-BE-561`) |\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | What is missing | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-564` | Whether the PATCH body may carry `tags`, and whether that replaces the tag set. US-002-02 says \"edit ... tags\" and AC-002-02-02 says tags are unchanged by a pinned-only PATCH; dedicated tag operations also exist. | FR-006, FR-010 | a contract decision |\n| `GAP-BE-565` | Success status of `API-NOTES-150096`. | FR-012 | a status decision |\n| `GAP-BE-566` | Whether an unknown tag filter or tag lifecycle in `search` expects tag rows to exist. Not stated. | `note_tags` index | a decision from `search` |\n| `GAP-BE-567` | Maximum number of tags per note and maximum title and body length. | data model | field-level requirements |\n| `GAP-BE-568` | Whether tag add or remove changes `updated_at`. Default in `ASM-BE-562`. | FR-010 to FR-012 | a product rule |\n| `GAP-BE-569` | No idempotency key or duplicate protection for `API-NOTES-232390`. | jobs | a contract decision |\n| `GAP-BE-570` | No list-notes operation is among this module's seven records, although `backend.overview` lists listing as a responsibility and BR-019 orders list results. Whether `search` serves the list or a list operation is missing is not stated. | `backend.overview` item 1 | a decision to add the operation or to confirm `search` serves it |\n| `GAP-BE-571` | Tag name format, case rules, and whether a tag with no remaining notes is kept. Default in `ASM-BE-564`, `ASM-BE-567`. | data model | field-level requirements |\n| `GAP-BE-572` | Response to a malformed note id (400 versus 404). | FR-005 | a status decision |\n| `GAP-BE-573` | Behaviour of removing an absent tag is not cited. Default in `ASM-BE-560`. | FR-012 | a status decision |\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-553` | Column types: uuid keys, text for strings, boolean, timestamptz. | PostgreSQL 16 is the platform and no types are supplied. | platform decision (`GAP-BE-089`) |\n| `ASM-BE-554` | A new note has `status` `active`. | US-002-04 archives an \"active, existing note\" and the create criteria carry no status. | confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-555` | The note id is server-assigned, opaque and never client-supplied. | AC-002-01-01 says the API returns a unique id. | confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-556` | A title or body of whitespace only counts as empty. | BR-005 rejects \"empty/null\" payloads; whitespace carries no content. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-557` | The non-empty rule for title and body also applies to PATCH. | BR-005 says \"at creation\" only; an edit that empties a field would lose content, against BR-006's intent. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-558` | Edit, pin and tag changes on an archived note behave as on an active note. | No input forbids them; BR-008 says only that unarchive restores the prior state. | `Q-BE-351` |\n| `ASM-BE-559` | Unarchive of an active note returns the archive conflict. | Mirrors US-002-04's second criterion; nothing else is cited. | `Q-BE-352` |\n| `ASM-BE-560` | Removing a tag not on the note returns 404 `NOT_FOUND`. | Add is explicitly idempotent while removal has no criteria; 404 keeps the two distinguishable. | `Q-BE-353` |\n| `ASM-BE-561` | Concurrent PATCH is last-write-wins, atomic per request, with no version column. | US-005-03 allows last-write or conflict and specifies neither further. | `Q-BE-354` |\n| `ASM-BE-562` | `updated_at` is set on PATCH, archive, unarchive and any tag change that alters the tag set. A repeated add of an existing tag, and a refused request, change nothing. | AC-002-02-01 requires an updated timestamp on edit only. | product owner (`GAP-BE-568`) |\n| `ASM-BE-563` | Tag and archive operations on an unknown note id return 404 `NOT_FOUND`. | US-002-02, US-002-05 and US-002-06 return 404 for unknown ids. | confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-564` | A `tags` row is created on first add and kept when no note carries it. | No input says tags are removed. | `GAP-BE-571` |\n| `ASM-BE-565` | No delete operation for notes is in scope. \"Deleted\" in US-002-06 is treated as never-existing. | BR-007 keeps data; no input describes a delete operation. | `CON-BE-300` resolution |\n| `ASM-BE-566` | `note_tags.note_id` is `ON DELETE CASCADE`. | If a retention deletion is ever approved, it must leave no dangling links. Dormant until `CON-BE-300` is resolved. | `CON-BE-300` resolution |\n| `ASM-BE-567` | Tag names are compared exactly, character for character. | No case or normalisation rule is stated. | `GAP-BE-571` |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-002-01", "US-002-02", "US-002-03", "US-002-04", "US-002-05", "US-002-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/search-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/search-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.search.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: search\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [CON-BE-003, Q-BE-003, Q-BE-012]\nscope: [API-SEARCH-580505]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `search` module is the read-only query path over notes. [backend.module-map; stories.md EP-003] It provides one operation, `API-SEARCH-580505`, which:\n\n1. Finds notes by keyword across title and body. [stories.md US-003-01]\n2. Filters notes by one or more tags. [stories.md US-003-02; BR-010]\n3. Combines keyword and tag filters in one request. [stories.md US-003-04; BR-010]\n4. Paginates results and returns a total count. Unbounded result sets are not permitted. [stories.md US-003-05; BR-011]\n5. Excludes archived notes by default and includes them only when the caller asks. [stories.md US-003-06; BR-009]\n6. Returns pinned notes before unpinned notes, then orders by relevance. [stories.md US-003-01; BR-019; INV-BE-001]\n7. Meets p95 latency under 300 ms at 50 requests per second, and answers 503 with a retry-after header when the search index is degraded or unavailable. [requirements.md NFR-006, NFR-007; stories.md US-003-03]\n\n**Stories owned.** US-003-01, US-003-02, US-003-03, US-003-04, US-003-05, US-003-06.\n\n**Operation owned.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, referenced by id only.\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Owning, creating, editing, pinning, tagging, archiving or deleting notes, tags or their links. Those belong to `notes`. Search never writes. [backend.module-map]\n- The note schema, the tag schema and the status values. They are defined in `backend.domain-model` and the `notes` module and are not restated here.\n- Authentication and the credential. `auth` issues the credential (`Q-BE-012`). Search only requires that a caller be authenticated.\n- The note access rule and ownership (`CON-BE-003`, `Q-BE-003`).\n- The global error envelope and error-code registry (`backend.error-model`).\n- The OpenAPI document and its example payloads (`api-docs`). [requirements.md NFR-009]\n- The web-app search box and its messages. [stories.md US-006-03]\n- Deleting notes under NFR-019 (`notes`, `CON-BE-300`).\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-603` onward, `CON-BE-401`, `GAP-BE-614` onward, `INV-BE-112` onward, `Q-BE-401` onward. Other ids are cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**Tables owned: none.** Search reads data owned by `notes` and defines no table, column or constraint of its own. [backend.module-map] Column and entity names below are the logical names in `backend.domain-model` (NOTE, TAG, NOTE_TAG). Physical names are defined by the `notes` module.\n\n**Indexes.** Search specifies the indexes it needs. They are created by project migration 003 `search-indexes`, which is reversible by dropping the indexes with no data loss. [backend.migration-plan] The requirement behind them is indexed lookup at 100k notes. [stories.md US-003-03] Index kinds are proposals (`ASM-BE-609`).\n\n| Index (on notes-owned tables) | Query it serves |\n|---|---|\n| Full-text index over note title and body | The keyword predicate (`q`) and the relevance rank. |\n| NOTE_TAG lookup on (tag name, note id) | The tag predicate, including the all-tags-present test (`ASM-BE-607`). |\n| NOTE ordering index on (status, pinned descending, note id) | The default `status = active` filter, pinned-first ordering and the deterministic tie-break (`ASM-BE-605`). |\n\n**Time and PII.** Search stores no timestamps and no PII. Note title and body may contain personal data, but no input classifies them (`GAP-BE-017`, cited from the project spec). The treatment of search terms in logs is `GAP-BE-624`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nSearch owns no entity with a status column, so there is no persistent state machine. The only state it observes is the note status (`active` or `archived`), which `notes` owns. This section therefore describes the lifecycle of one request. Each request is independent and no state survives it.\n\n**Request lifecycle.** Terminal outcomes are marked.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Result |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| received | rejected-auth (terminal) | request arrives | no valid credential | 401 (cited in `backend.error-model`). Checked first (`ASM-BE-611`). |\n| received | rejected-forbidden (terminal) | request arrives | credential valid but not permitted | 403. Blocked on `CON-BE-003`. |\n| received | rejected-validation (terminal) | authenticated | any parameter fails validation | 400. No query runs and no partial data is returned (INV-BE-117). |\n| received | executing | authenticated and valid | | query runs against the index |\n| executing | unavailable (terminal) | index degraded or unavailable | `GAP-BE-619` | 503 with a retry-after header. [stories.md US-003-03] |\n| executing | answered-empty (terminal) | zero matches, or page beyond the last page | | 200 with an empty array. [stories.md US-003-01, US-003-02, US-003-05] |\n| executing | answered (terminal) | one or more matches on the page | | 200 with the page and the total count |\n\n**Index availability** is derived per request as available or unavailable. It is not stored, and the 503 trigger is `GAP-BE-619` and `ASM-BE-610`.\n\n**Note status as a filter input.** Default requests see only `active` notes. With `include_archived=true` both `active` and `archived` notes are visible. [requirements.md BR-009] Search never changes a status.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\nParameter names `q`, `tags`, `page`, `limit` and `include_archived` are cited from US-003-01 to US-003-06. Their value syntax is not fully cited (see `backend.search.gaps`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-001 Keyword search\n**Given** active notes whose title or body match the keyword,\n**When** `API-SEARCH-580505` is called with `q=keyword`,\n**Then** the matching notes are returned with status 200.\n- **Matching rule.** Case-insensitive match against title and body (`ASM-BE-608`, `GAP-BE-616`).\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-keyword-matches-title-and-body` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-002 Pinned first, then relevance\n**Given** a result set containing pinned and unpinned notes,\n**When** `API-SEARCH-580505` returns it,\n**Then** every pinned note precedes every unpinned note, and the order within each group is by relevance when `q` is supplied (`ASM-BE-613`), with remaining ties broken by `ASM-BE-605`.\n- **Open point.** BR-019 says ordering within a pinned group is unchanged, while US-003-01 says relevance. See `GAP-BE-310` (cited from the project spec).\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01; BR-019; INV-BE-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-pinned-before-unpinned`, `test-search-relevance-within-group` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-003 No keyword match\n**Given** no note matches the keyword,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** 200 is returned with an empty array and no error.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-keyword-no-match-empty-200` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-004 Missing criteria\n**Given** a request with no non-empty `q` and no non-empty `tags`,\n**When** `API-SEARCH-580505` is called,\n**Then** 400 is returned with a validation error and no query runs.\n- **Reading used.** `q` is required unless `tags` is supplied (`CON-BE-401`, `Q-BE-401`).\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01, US-003-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-missing-q-and-tags-400`, `test-search-empty-q-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-005 Tag filter\n**Given** notes tagged `project-x`,\n**When** the search is called with `tags=project-x`,\n**Then** only notes carrying that tag are returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02; BR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-tag-filter-only-matching` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-006 Several tags\n**Given** a request listing more than one tag,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** a note matches only when it carries every listed tag (`ASM-BE-607`, `Q-BE-402`). The syntax is `ASM-BE-612`.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02 (\"one or more tags\").\n- **Verification.** `test-search-multiple-tags-all-required` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-007 Tag with no notes\n**Given** a tag that no note carries,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** 200 is returned with an empty result set.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-unknown-tag-empty-200` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-008 Invalid tag characters\n**Given** a `tags` value containing invalid characters,\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** 400 is returned with an error detail.\n- **Open point.** The valid tag character set is not defined (`GAP-BE-617`; tag format also in `GAP-BE-035`, cited).\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-invalid-tag-chars-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-009 Combined filters\n**Given** `q=budget` and `tags=finance` supplied together,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** only notes matching both criteria are returned. If the tag filter excludes every keyword match, 200 is returned with an empty set.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-04; BR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-combined-keyword-and-tag`, `test-search-combined-excludes-all-empty` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-010 Malformed combination\n**Given** a malformed filter combination,\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** 400 is returned with error details.\n- **Open point.** What counts as malformed beyond FR-004, FR-008, FR-013 and FR-016 is `GAP-BE-618`.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-malformed-combination-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-011 Pagination\n**Given** more matches than the page size,\n**When** `page` and `limit` are supplied,\n**Then** the requested subset is returned together with the total count of all matching notes.\n- **Page base.** Pages start at 1 (`ASM-BE-604`). The default page size is not stated (`GAP-BE-500`, cited). A limit above the maximum is `GAP-BE-614`.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05; BR-011.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-pagination-subset-and-total` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-012 Page beyond results\n**Given** a page number beyond the available results,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** 200 is returned with an empty array. The total count is still reported (INV-BE-114).\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-page-beyond-last-empty-200` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-013 Invalid page or limit\n**Given** a negative or non-numeric `page` or `limit`,\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** 400 is returned with a validation error. Zero and over-maximum values are `GAP-BE-614`.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-negative-page-400`, `test-search-non-numeric-limit-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-014 Archived excluded by default\n**Given** both archived and active notes exist,\n**When** a default search is executed,\n**Then** only active notes are returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06; BR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-default-excludes-archived` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-015 Include archived\n**Given** `include_archived=true`,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** archived notes are included alongside active notes.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06; BR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-include-archived-true` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-016 Invalid include_archived\n**Given** an `include_archived` value that is not a valid boolean (`ASM-BE-606`, `GAP-BE-620`),\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** 400 is returned with an error message.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-invalid-include-archived-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-017 Latency\n**Given** 50 requests per second against a dataset of about 100k notes,\n**When** searches are executed through the indexes in `backend.search.data-model`,\n**Then** p95 latency is under 300 ms, and still under 300 ms at page sizes up to 100.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-03; NFR-006; NFR-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-load-p95-under-300ms-50rps`, `test-search-100k-notes-indexed` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-018 Index unavailable\n**Given** the search index is degraded or unavailable,\n**When** a search is requested,\n**Then** 503 is returned with a retry-after header instead of hanging.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-index-unavailable-503-retry-after` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Open point.** `GAP-BE-619` and `ASM-BE-610`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-019 Injection safety\n**Given** keyword and tag values containing query metacharacters,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** the values are treated as data only and cause no injection.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-injection-payloads-neutral`, security scan per release (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-020 Deterministic paging\n**Given** unchanged data and any fixed request,\n**When** consecutive pages are requested,\n**Then** the pages are disjoint, in a stable order, and their union is the full result (INV-BE-115).\n- **Traces to.** Derived from US-003-05 and BR-011, because an undefined order (`GAP-BE-310`) would make paging unreliable. Not cited in an input.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-pages-disjoint-and-stable` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-021 Authenticated callers only\n**Given** a request without a valid credential,\n**When** `API-SEARCH-580505` is called,\n**Then** it is refused before validation (`ASM-BE-611`).\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` on `Q-BE-012` (credential form) and `CON-BE-003` (who may search).\n- **Traces to.** Derived from `backend.auth-model`, which grants search to an authenticated team member under the `Q-BE-001` default.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-unauthenticated-401` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### Story coverage\n\n| Story | Requirements |\n|---|---|\n| US-003-01 | BE-SEARCH-FR-001, 002, 003, 004 |\n| US-003-02 | BE-SEARCH-FR-004, 005, 006, 007, 008 |\n| US-003-03 | BE-SEARCH-FR-017, 018 |\n| US-003-04 | BE-SEARCH-FR-009, 010 |\n| US-003-05 | BE-SEARCH-FR-011, 012, 013, 020 |\n| US-003-06 | BE-SEARCH-FR-014, 015, 016 |\n\nAll six owned stories are covered.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | GET | `/notes/search` | `search` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 500, 503 | Search notes by keyword and/or tags with pagination, an archived filter, and pinned notes returned before unpinned notes (US-003-01 to US-003-06; BR-009 to BR-011, BR-019). |\n\n### `API-SEARCH-580505` \u2014 GET `/notes/search`\n\nSearch notes by keyword and/or tags with pagination, an archived filter, and pinned notes returned before unpinned notes (US-003-01 to US-003-06; BR-009 to BR-011, BR-019).\n\n**Request:** \n- `q` (string, required): Keyword query matched against notes. A missing or empty value returns a validation error (US-003-01 to US-003-06).\n- `tags` (array): Tag names to filter by. Invalid tags or a malformed filter return a validation error (US-003-02, US-003-04).\n- `include_archived` (boolean): When true, archived notes are included in results. Archived notes are excluded by default. An invalid value returns a validation error (US-003-06, BR-009).\n- `page` (integer): Page number of results. A bad value returns a validation error (US-003-05, BR-011).\n- `limit` (integer): Page size, up to 100 (NFR-007). The default and maximum are not stated (GAP-BE-500). A bad value returns a validation error.\n**Response:** \n- `results` (array, required): Matching notes for the requested page, every pinned note before every unpinned note (INV-BE-001). An empty array with 200 when nothing matches (US-003-01). The within-group order is undefined (GAP-BE-310). The field name is proposed, since no input names it.\n- `page` (integer): Page number of the returned page. Proposed field, not named in any input.\n- `limit` (integer): Page size applied to the returned page. Proposed field, not named in any input.\n- `total` (integer): Total number of matching notes across all pages. Proposed field, not named in any input.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\nThese hold after every call to `API-SEARCH-580505`. `INV-BE-001` (pinned before unpinned in search results) is defined in `backend.domain-model` and applies here unchanged.\n\n- `INV-BE-112`. Without `include_archived=true`, no archived note appears in the results. [requirements.md BR-009]\n- `INV-BE-113`. The operation changes no stored data. [backend.module-map]\n- `INV-BE-114`. The reported total count is the number of notes matching the filters, independent of `page` and `limit`.\n- `INV-BE-115`. For unchanged data and fixed parameters, pages are disjoint, stably ordered, and together equal the full result (`ASM-BE-605`).\n- `INV-BE-116`. A response never carries more notes than the maximum page size. [requirements.md BR-011] The value is `GAP-BE-500` (cited) and `ASM-BE-603`.\n- `INV-BE-117`. A request that fails validation returns no note data and runs no query.\n- `INV-BE-118`. A note appears in the results only if it matches every supplied filter (keyword and every listed tag).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\nNo input defines roles, scopes or a permission model. A role has no permission on an operation unless an input grants it (`ASM-BE-302`, cited from the project spec).\n\n| Operation | Unauthenticated caller | Authenticated team member | Other actors |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | Not stated, so denied | Allow under the `Q-BE-001` default, `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-003` | Integration engineer [stories.md US-003-03]: not stated. QA engineer: not stated. |\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None can be written. No input relates a note to a user (`GAP-BE-003`, cited). Under the `Q-BE-001` default every authenticated team member sees all notes. If `CON-BE-003` is resolved otherwise, the predicate must be applied inside the search query before pagination and before the total count. A filter applied afterwards would leak counts and break paging.\n\n**Dependencies.** The credential form is `Q-BE-012`. The existing authentication method is `GAP-BE-085`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.jobs | status: DERIVED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n**Scheduled work: none.** Search owns no data, so it has nothing to sweep, expire or rebuild. [backend.module-map] Retention deletion of notes belongs to `notes` and is blocked on `CON-BE-300`.\n\n**Index upkeep.** Index maintenance is a consequence of writes made by `notes`. How soon a changed note becomes searchable is `GAP-BE-625`.\n\n**Retries.** The server retries nothing. A caller that receives 503 retries after the retry-after interval (`GAP-BE-619`).\n\n**Idempotency.** The operation is a GET and is safe to repeat (INV-BE-113). No idempotency key applies.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\nAll tests are planned. Names match the Verification lines in `backend.search.requirements`.\n\n**Unit.**\n\n- Parameter parsing and validation: `q`, `tags` (syntax `ASM-BE-612`), `page`, `limit`, `include_archived`. This covers every 400 path (FR-004, 008, 010, 013, 016).\n- Ordering comparator: pinned first, then relevance, then the id tie-break (FR-002, FR-020).\n- Total-count and page-slice arithmetic, including a page beyond the last (FR-011, FR-012).\n- Query construction uses bound parameters only (FR-019).\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16 [platform.md]).\n\n- Seed active, archived, pinned and tagged notes. Cover FR-001 to FR-003, FR-005 to FR-009, FR-014, FR-015.\n- Boundary data: a tag with no notes, a keyword with no match, and a page beyond the results.\n- Concurrent-write consistency: results read during a note edit return either the old or the new note, never a partial one. The freshness target is `GAP-BE-625`.\n- Index outage simulation returns 503 with a retry-after header (FR-018).\n- Performance: 100k notes, 50 requests per second, p95 under 300 ms, and again at page size 100 (FR-017).\n- Authentication refusal before validation (FR-021). This is blocked on `Q-BE-012` and `CON-BE-003`.\n\n**Contract.**\n\n- Responses match the OpenAPI document published by `api-docs` for `API-SEARCH-580505`. This includes the 400, 503 and authentication error statuses and the retry-after header. [stories.md US-004-02, US-004-03]\n- Example payloads exist for a populated result and an empty result. [requirements.md NFR-009; stories.md US-004-05]\n- The scope-guardrail suite finds no endpoint under search that matches an excluded category. [requirements.md NFR-010]\n\n**Security.** Injection payload corpus on `q` and `tags`. 0 critical findings per release. [requirements.md NFR-012]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions, assumptions and questions\n\n### Contradiction checks\n\n- **Story to design.** The design brief (AC-001-01-01 to AC-002-01-03) has no acceptance criteria for EP-003, so this check could not be performed for search (`GAP-BE-622`).\n- **Story to requirement.** No contradiction found for the search stories against BR-009 to BR-011 and BR-019, apart from the open ordering point `GAP-BE-310` (cited).\n- **Requirement to requirement.** BR-019 and US-003-01 differ on ordering within a pinned group. This is recorded as `GAP-BE-310` in the project spec and is not repeated here.\n- **Story to story.** `CON-BE-401` below.\n- **Design to design.** Not applicable.\n- **Cited, not minted.** `CON-BE-003` (note access, Critical) and `CON-BE-300` (NFR-019 deletes notes after 1 day, Critical) both affect what a search may return. This module is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-003` for authorization only. All other sections are written independently of it.\n\n### Contradiction\n\n| ID | Severity | Statement |\n|---|---|---|\n| `CON-BE-401` | non-critical | US-003-01 returns 400 when `q` is missing or empty. US-003-02's example (`GET /notes/search?tags=project-x`) and US-003-06's default search supply no `q`. Reading used: `q` is required unless `tags` is supplied (FR-004). Consequence of the other side: if `q` is always required, tag-only filtering is impossible. It is non-critical because it changes a query parameter rule, not a data shape, state machine, auth rule or money/PII path. |\n\n### Questions (all degradable, defaults applied because the run is unattended)\n\n| ID | Class | Question and default |\n|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-401` | degradable | Is a tag-only search (no `q`) allowed? Default: yes (`CON-BE-401`). |\n| `Q-BE-402` | degradable | With several tags, must a note carry all of them or any of them? Default: all (`ASM-BE-607`). |\n| `Q-BE-403` | degradable | Is an archived-only view needed? Default: no such filter (`GAP-BE-623`). |\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | What is missing | Degrades | Fallback used | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-614` | Behaviour for `page=0`, `limit=0`, and a `limit` above the maximum (reject with 400 or clamp). US-003-05 rejects only negative and non-numeric values. | FR-011, FR-013 | Reject 0 as invalid. Over-maximum is not decided. | Pagination decision (with `GAP-BE-500`, cited) |\n| `GAP-BE-615` | Constraints on `q`: minimum and maximum length, whitespace-only values, trimming. | FR-001, FR-004 | None applied. | Field-level requirements |\n| `GAP-BE-616` | Keyword matching semantics: whole word or substring, stemming, language configuration, multi-word handling. | FR-001, FR-002 | `ASM-BE-608`. | Search behaviour decision |\n| `GAP-BE-617` | `tags` syntax (separator, repeated parameter), case sensitivity, valid character set, and the maximum number of tags. | FR-005, FR-006, FR-008 | `ASM-BE-612`. | Tag format decision (also `GAP-BE-035`, cited) |\n| `GAP-BE-618` | What counts as a \"malformed filter combination\" beyond the other 400 cases. | FR-010 | Only the cases in FR-004, 008, 013 and 016 are rejected. | Field-level requirements |\n| `GAP-BE-619` | What \"degraded\" means for the search index, and the retry-after value. | FR-018 | `ASM-BE-610`. No retry-after value is stated. | Operations decision |\n| `GAP-BE-620` | Accepted forms of `include_archived` (case, `1` and `0`, `yes`) and the default when absent. | FR-016 | `ASM-BE-606`. | Contract decision |\n| `GAP-BE-621` | Rate-limit class for the operation. | Contract | None. | Rate-limit policy |\n| `GAP-BE-622` | No design-brief criteria for EP-003. | The story-to-design check | Stories and requirements only. | Acceptance criteria for US-003-01 to US-003-06 |\n| `GAP-BE-623` | No way to list archived notes only, although the web-app archived-notes view needs it. [stories.md US-006-05] `include_archived=true` returns active and archived notes together. | Cross-surface | Not provided. | `Q-BE-403` answer. A web-app need for backend. |\n| `GAP-BE-624` | Whether search terms may be logged. NFR-018 covers email addresses and codes only, and notes may hold personal data. | `backend.observability` | Nothing claimed. | Data-protection policy (with `GAP-BE-017`, cited) |\n| `GAP-BE-625` | How soon a created or edited note must be visible to search. | Consistency tests | None. | Freshness target |\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption and reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-603` | The maximum `limit` is 100. NFR-007 states latency \"up to page sizes of 100\" but not a maximum. | Pagination decision (`GAP-BE-500`, `GAP-BE-614`) |\n| `ASM-BE-604` | `page` starts at 1. No input states the base. | Contract decision |\n| `ASM-BE-605` | The final sort key is the note id, which makes paging deterministic. Required because the within-group order is undefined (`GAP-BE-310`). | Ordering decision |\n| `ASM-BE-606` | `include_archived` accepts only `true` or `false`. Only `true` is cited. | `GAP-BE-620` |\n| `ASM-BE-607` | Several tags mean \"all must be present\". US-003-02 says filtering \"narrows\" results. | `Q-BE-402` |\n| `ASM-BE-608` | A keyword matches a note when its terms appear, case-insensitively, in title or body. Stories say \"across title and content\" only. | `GAP-BE-616` |\n| `ASM-BE-609` | The search index is PostgreSQL indexes (full-text and btree), since the platform names no other store. [platform.md] | Platform decision |\n| `ASM-BE-610` | 503 is returned when the database or its search indexes cannot serve the query. This is distinct from an unhandled failure (500). | `GAP-BE-619` |\n| `ASM-BE-611` | Authentication is checked before parameter validation. This is not cited and keeps unauthenticated callers from probing the parameters. | Contract decision |\n| `ASM-BE-612` | `tags` is one parameter with comma-separated values, matching the `tags=project-x` form. | `GAP-BE-617` |\n| `ASM-BE-613` | Relevance ordering applies only when `q` is supplied. A tag-only search has no relevance score. | `GAP-BE-310` (cited) |\n\n### Items cited from the project spec (not minted here)\n\n`GAP-BE-003`, `GAP-BE-017`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-310`, `GAP-BE-500`, `ASM-BE-302`, `CON-BE-003`, `CON-BE-300`, `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-003`, `Q-BE-012`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-003-01", "US-003-02", "US-003-03", "US-003-04", "US-003-05", "US-003-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/residency-compliance-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/residency-compliance-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: residency-compliance\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [CON-BE-151, Q-BE-152, Q-BE-151, GAP-BE-305, GAP-BE-308]\nscope: [API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-548093, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `residency-compliance` module is the application-side record and checker for EU data residency of notes_api infrastructure. [backend.module-map; stories.md EP-001] It owns:\n\n1. **Region check.** It decides whether supplied database, compute and backup-storage resources are in approved EU regions. It fails closed when region metadata is missing or unreadable. Operation `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596`. [US-001-01, US-001-03; BR-001, BR-002; AC-001-03-03]\n2. **Backup validation.** It rejects a backup configuration whose destination is outside the EU, within 30 seconds, with a residency violation error and no data written. Operation `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124`. [US-001-02; AC-001-02-01, AC-001-02-02]\n3. **Compliance report.** It lists every component with its region and pass/fail/unverified status, plus the overall percent compliant. Unrecognised resource types are flagged `unverified`, never omitted. Operation `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995`. [US-001-04; AC-001-04-01, AC-001-04-02; NFR-001, NFR-004]\n4. **Launch gate.** It derives gate status from the report. Operation `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`. [US-001-05; BR-003; AC-001-05-01, AC-001-05-02]\n5. **Launch sign-off.** It records the Engineering Lead sign-off. Operation `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-548093`. It is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152`. [BR-003]\n6. **Drift scan and alerts.** It runs a periodic scan plus an on-demand trigger (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`). It logs every scan result, raises an alert for any running resource outside an EU region within 15 minutes of detection, and lists alerts (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`). [US-001-06; BR-004; NFR-003; AC-001-06-01, AC-001-06-02]\n7. **Data ownership.** It owns INFRA_RESOURCE and DRIFT_ALERT data, and the scan, approved-region and sign-off records below. [backend.module-map, backend.domain-model]\n\n**Stories owned.** US-001-01, US-001-02, US-001-03, US-001-04, US-001-05, US-001-06.\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- **Provisioning.** It creates no resource. The rejection of a non-EU provisioning request (AC-001-01-02) is enforced by infrastructure-as-code region constraints. [BR-001 Enforcement]\n- **Backups.** It does not run backup jobs or write backup artefacts. It validates a configuration only. Frequency and retention are `GAP-BE-307`.\n- **The CI/CD pipeline.** It does not run the pipeline or block a build. The pipeline step consumes the verdict of `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596`. [BR-002]\n- **Email transport region.** That is `Q-BE-005` and `CON-BE-006` in `auth`.\n- **Other modules.** Notes, search, login, the OpenAPI document and the scope guardrail belong to their own modules. The global error envelope is defined once in `backend.error-model`.\n- **Role model.** Roles and credentials are `GAP-BE-085` and `Q-BE-012`.\n\n**Operations owned.** The seven ids in the frontmatter, referenced by id only.\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-653` onward, `GAP-BE-664` onward, `INV-BE-162` onward. It mints no `CON-` and no `Q-`. Other ids are cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\nNo input says where residency data lives (`GAP-BE-305`). The default is the notes_api PostgreSQL 16 database in an EU region (`ASM-BE-653`). Types are a proposal (`ASM-BE-654`). Entity names follow `backend.domain-model`. This module adds `approved_regions`, `scan_runs` and `launch_gate_signoffs`. All three are proposals.\n\n**approved_regions**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `region` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `added_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n\nThe table is empty until `Q-BE-151` is answered. No region name is assumed (`ASM-BE-655`).\n\n**infra_resources**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | service-generated | primary key |\n| `external_ref` | text | NOT NULL | none | unique; the resource's identifier in the hosting platform |\n| `resource_type` | text | NOT NULL | none | no enumerated CHECK, so an unrecognised type can be stored (`ASM-BE-657`) |\n| `region` | text | NULL | none | null means missing or unreadable |\n| `compliance_status` | text | NOT NULL | `'unverified'` | `CHECK (compliance_status IN ('pass','fail','unverified'))` (`ASM-BE-656`) |\n| `last_scanned_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | |\n| `first_seen_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n\n- **Pass constraint.** `CHECK (compliance_status <> 'pass' OR region IS NOT NULL)`. A foreign key from `region` to `approved_regions` is deliberately absent, because a non-EU region must still be recordable as `fail`. \"Pass only if the region is approved\" is enforced in the service (`INV-BE-162`).\n- **Index.** `external_ref` unique serves the upsert on each scan. btree `(compliance_status)` serves the report counts.\n\n**scan_runs**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `scan_run_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | service-generated | primary key |\n| `trigger` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (trigger IN ('scheduled','on-demand'))` |\n| `state` | text | NOT NULL | `'RUNNING'` | `CHECK (state IN ('RUNNING','COMPLETED','FAILED'))` |\n| `started_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `finished_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | `CHECK (finished_at IS NULL OR finished_at >= started_at)` |\n| `resources_scanned` | integer | NULL | none | `CHECK (resources_scanned >= 0)` |\n| `non_eu_found` | integer | NULL | none | `CHECK (non_eu_found >= 0)` |\n\n- **Outcome.** A run's outcome (pass or findings) is derived from `non_eu_found` and is logged on every run, including a pass. [AC-001-06-01]\n- **Indexes.** btree `(started_at DESC)` serves the latest-run lookup. A unique partial index on a constant expression `WHERE state = 'RUNNING'` allows one `RUNNING` row (`ASM-BE-663`).\n\n**drift_alerts**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `alert_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | service-generated | primary key |\n| `scan_run_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `scan_runs`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `infra_resources`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n| `detected_region` | text | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `detected_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `raised_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (raised_at >= detected_at)` |\n| `state` | text | NOT NULL | `'OPEN'` | `CHECK (state IN ('OPEN','CLOSED'))` (`ASM-BE-662`) |\n| `closed_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | `CHECK ((state = 'CLOSED') = (closed_at IS NOT NULL))` |\n| `delivered_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | channel and recipients are `GAP-BE-308` |\n\n- **Indexes.** A unique partial index on `(resource_id) WHERE state = 'OPEN'` enforces one open alert per resource (`INV-BE-168`). btree `(raised_at DESC)` serves the alert list.\n- **Delete behaviour.** `RESTRICT` on both keys, because no input permits deleting alert history. Retention is `GAP-BE-666`.\n\n**launch_gate_signoffs**: the whole table is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152`. It is described only so the migration can be sized.\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `signoff_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | service-generated | primary key |\n| `signed_by` | text | NOT NULL | none | identity of the Engineering Lead; form depends on `Q-BE-012` and `GAP-BE-085` |\n| `signed_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `basis_scan_run_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `scan_runs`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n| `report_percent` | numeric(5,2) | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (report_percent = 100.00)`; a sign-off is only recordable against a passing report |\n\n**Time.** Every instant is `timestamptz`.\n\n**PII and encryption.** `signed_by` identifies a person and is personal data (as `ASM-BE-019`). Every other column holds infrastructure identifiers. No input states encryption at rest (`GAP-BE-304`), so none is claimed. No credential or secret is stored in any column.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nFour lifecycles. Statuses for refusals are `GAP-BE-665`. Error code names are proposals (`ASM-BE-301`).\n\n### 3.1 Resource compliance status (`infra_resources.compliance_status`)\n\nStates: `unverified` (initial), `pass`, `fail`. None is terminal; every scan may move a resource.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| (new) | `unverified` | scan finds a resource of an unrecognised type | type not in `ASM-BE-657` | row created; flagged in the report [AC-001-04-02] |\n| (new) or any | `pass` | scan reads the region | region is in `approved_regions` and type is recognised | `last_scanned_at` set |\n| (new) or any | `fail` | scan reads the region | region not in `approved_regions` | `last_scanned_at` set; drift alert if guards in 3.3 hold |\n| (new) or any | `fail` | scan cannot read the region | region metadata missing or unreadable | `region` stored null; no alert (`ASM-BE-661`, `GAP-BE-669`) |\n| any | `unverified` | scan reads an unrecognised type | type not in `ASM-BE-657` | never counted as `pass` (`INV-BE-163`) |\n\n### 3.2 Scan run (`scan_runs.state`)\n\nStates: `RUNNING`, `COMPLETED` (terminal), `FAILED` (terminal).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| (new) | `RUNNING` | schedule fires or `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` called | no other run is `RUNNING` (`ASM-BE-663`) | row created |\n| `RUNNING` | `COMPLETED` | every resource observed | | counts and `finished_at` set; result logged, pass included [AC-001-06-01] |\n| `RUNNING` | `FAILED` | scan cannot enumerate resources | | `finished_at` set; error logged; the next scheduled run is the retry |\n\nA trigger while a run is `RUNNING` is refused. Status is `GAP-BE-665` and the idempotency position is `ASM-BE-665`.\n\n### 3.3 Drift alert (`drift_alerts.state`)\n\nStates: `OPEN`, `CLOSED` (terminal). Open and close rules are proposals (`ASM-BE-662`).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| (new) | `OPEN` | scan reads a resource outside every approved region | no `OPEN` alert exists for the resource | alert created with `detected_region` and resource details; raised within 15 minutes of detection [NFR-003, BR-004] |\n| `OPEN` | `CLOSED` | a later scan reads the resource in an approved region, or finds it gone | | `closed_at` set |\n\nA recurrence after `CLOSED` creates a new alert row.\n\n### 3.4 Launch gate (derived, not stored)\n\nStates: `PASSED`, `FAILED`. The gate is computed at request time (`ASM-BE-659`) and is not a stored row.\n\n| Condition | Gate status | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| latest scan run is `COMPLETED` and percent compliant is 100 (`ASM-BE-658`) | `PASSED` | AC-001-05-01 |\n| anything else, including no scan run, no resources, or any `fail` or `unverified` resource | `FAILED` | AC-001-05-02, `INV-BE-165` |\n\n**Sign-off (`BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-151`, `Q-BE-152`).** BR-003 enforces the launch gate by Engineering Lead sign-off. AC-001-05-01 marks the gate passed and release authorised from the report alone. Which reading holds is a human decision, and this document does not choose. The only certain guard is that a sign-off is refused while the gate is `FAILED`. Whether a `PASSED` gate alone authorises release, or needs the sign-off record, is open.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\nWhether a region is \"approved\" depends on `approved_regions`, which is empty until `Q-BE-151` is answered.
