Every `pass` outcome below is therefore `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED` until then (`ASM-BE-655`).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-001 Region check passes for EU resources\n**Given** a set of database, compute and backup-storage resources whose regions are all in `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596` is called,\n**Then** every resource is reported `pass`, the overall verdict is pass, and the deployment may proceed.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01; US-001-03; AC-001-01-01; AC-001-03-01; BR-001; BR-002.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-check-all-eu-passes` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596`.\n- **Status.** The region list is `Q-BE-151`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-002 Region check fails on a non-EU resource\n**Given** at least one supplied resource whose region is not in `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596` is called,\n**Then** that resource is reported `fail`, the overall verdict is fail, and the response names the failing resources so the pipeline can block the deployment.\n- **Provisioning.** Rejection of the provisioning request itself belongs to infrastructure-as-code constraints, not this module (purpose section).\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01; US-001-03; AC-001-01-02; AC-001-03-02; BR-001; BR-002; NFR-002.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-check-non-eu-fails` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596`. Failing-verdict status is `GAP-BE-665`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-003 Fail closed on missing or unreadable region\n**Given** a supplied resource whose region metadata is missing or unreadable,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596` is called,\n**Then** that resource is treated as `fail`, never as a default pass, and the overall verdict is fail.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-03; INV-BE-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-check-missing-region-fails` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-004 Region check is stateless\n**Given** any call to `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596`,\n**When** it completes,\n**Then** no row of `infra_resources`, `scan_runs` or `drift_alerts` has changed, and one log entry records the verdict.\n- **Traces to.** Derived: US-001-04 states the report reflects resources that \"have been scanned\", so scans populate the records (`ASM-BE-660`).\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-check-persists-nothing` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-005 Accept an EU backup destination\n**Given** a backup configuration whose destination region is in `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124` is called,\n**Then** validation succeeds and nothing is written.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-02; AC-001-02-01; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-backup-validation-eu-accepted` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-006 Reject a non-EU backup destination\n**Given** a backup configuration whose destination is outside every approved region, or whose region cannot be read,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124` is called,\n**Then** within 30 seconds of receipt the request is rejected with a residency violation error and no data is written (`ASM-BE-664`).\n- **Traces to.** US-001-02; AC-001-02-02; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-backup-validation-non-eu-rejected`, `test-backup-validation-under-30s` (planned, injectable clock).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124`. Status and error code are `GAP-BE-665`, `ASM-BE-301`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-007 Compliance report content\n**Given** resources exist and have been scanned,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` is called,\n**Then** the report lists each resource, its region, and a pass/fail/unverified status, plus the overall percent compliant (`ASM-BE-658`).\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-01; NFR-001; NFR-004.\n- **Verification.** `test-report-lists-all-with-percent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995`.\n- **Status.** Storage of report data is `GAP-BE-305` and `ASM-BE-653`. Report freshness is `GAP-BE-667`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-008 Unrecognised type is flagged, not omitted\n**Given** a resource whose type the scanner does not recognise,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` is called,\n**Then** the resource appears with status `unverified`, and is counted in the percent-compliant denominator and never as `pass`.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-report-unrecognised-type-unverified` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-009 Launch gate passes on a 100% report\n**Given** the latest scan run is `COMPLETED` and the report shows 100% EU-verified resources,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` is called,\n**Then** the gate status is `PASSED`.\n- **Release authority.** Whether `PASSED` alone authorises release, or an Engineering Lead sign-off is also needed, is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152`.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-01; BR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-gate-passed-at-100-percent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-010 Launch gate fails below 100%\n**Given** the report shows less than 100% EU-verified resources, or there is no completed scan run or no resource,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` is called,\n**Then** the gate status is `FAILED` and release stays blocked until resolved.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-02; BR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-gate-failed-below-100-percent`, `test-gate-failed-no-evidence` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`. The no-evidence case is `ASM-BE-658`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-011 Record the Engineering Lead sign-off (`BLOCKED`)\n**Given** the gate is `PASSED` and the caller is the Engineering Lead,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-548093` is called,\n**Then** a sign-off row is recorded against the latest completed scan run.\n**Given** the gate is `FAILED`,\n**When** the same operation is called,\n**Then** it is refused and no row is written.\n- **Traces to.** BR-003; US-001-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-signoff-refused-while-failed` (planned). The success case is `BLOCKED`.\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-548093`.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152`. Only the refusal while `FAILED` is certain.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-012 Scan with no drift\n**Given** a scan run in which every resource is read in an approved region,\n**When** the run completes,\n**Then** no alert is raised and the run's pass result is logged.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-scan-all-eu-logs-pass-no-alert` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` (on-demand); the schedule is in the jobs section.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-013 Scan with drift raises an alert\n**Given** a scan finds a running resource in a non-EU region and no `OPEN` alert exists for it,\n**When** the run processes that resource,\n**Then** an alert with the resource's details (`resource_id`, `external_ref`, `resource_type`, `detected_region`) is created, and it is raised within 15 minutes of detection.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02; BR-004; NFR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-scan-drift-raises-alert`, `test-scan-drift-alert-within-15-min`, `test-scan-drift-no-duplicate-open-alert` (planned, injectable clock).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`.\n- **Status.** The alert channel and recipients are `GAP-BE-308`. US-001-06 says \"immediately\" and NFR-003 says within 15 minutes; the 15 minutes is read as the upper bound.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-014 List drift alerts\n**Given** drift alerts exist,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` is called,\n**Then** the alerts are returned with their resource details and state.\n- **Pagination and filtering.** Page size is not stated (`GAP-BE-500`).\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-drift-alerts-listed-with-details` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-015 Trigger an on-demand scan\n**Given** no scan run is `RUNNING`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` is called,\n**Then** a `RUNNING` scan run is created and processed as in FR-012 and FR-013.\n**Given** a scan run is `RUNNING`,\n**When** the same operation is called,\n**Then** it is refused (`ASM-BE-663`).\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; BR-004.\n- **Verification.** `test-scan-single-flight` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`. Status is `GAP-BE-665`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124` | POST | `/residency/backup-validations` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 422, 500 | Validate a backup configuration: a target outside the EU is rejected with a residency violation error within 30 seconds and no data is written (US-001-02, AC-001-02-01, AC-001-02-02). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` | GET | `/residency/compliance-report` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403, 500 | Return the current compliance report: every infrastructure component with its EU verification status, including unrecognised types flagged unverified, and the overall percent compliant (US-001-04, NFR-001, NFR-004). Alert channel and recipients are open (GAP-BE-308). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` | GET | `/residency/launch-gate` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403, 500 | Return the launch gate status derived from the compliance report (US-001-05, BR-003). Whether a pass authorises release or needs Engineering Lead sign-off is unresolved (CON-BE-151, Q-BE-152). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-548093` | POST | `/residency/launch-gate/signoff` | `residency-compliance` | 201 | 400, 401, 403, 409, 500 | Record the Engineering Lead sign-off authorising release (BR-003). Refused while the gate has failed. Blocked on CON-BE-151 and Q-BE-152. |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596` | POST | `/residency/region-checks` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 500 | Check that the supplied infrastructure resources (database, compute, backup storage) are in approved EU regions; fails closed when region metadata is missing or unreadable (US-001-01, US-001-03, BR-001, BR-002, INV-BE-007). Pipeline step may call it; approved region list is open (Q-BE-151). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` | POST | `/residency/scans` | `residency-compliance` | 202 | 400, 401, 403, 409, 500 | Trigger a residency drift scan of running resources; every scan result is logged and any resource outside an EU region raises a drift alert within 15 minutes of detection (US-001-06, BR-004, NFR-003). The periodic scan itself is a scheduled job, this is the on-demand trigger. |\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124` \u2014 POST `/residency/backup-validations`\n\nValidate a backup configuration: a target outside the EU is rejected with a residency violation error within 30 seconds and no data is written (US-001-02, AC-001-02-01, AC-001-02-02).\n\n**Request:** \n- `backup_target` (object, required): The backup configuration under validation, including the storage location and its region.\n- `resource_id` (string): Identifier of the backed-up resource, if known.\n**Response:** \n- `valid` (boolean, required): True when the backup storage is in an approved EU region.\n- `region` (string, required): Region the backup target resolves to.\n- `validated_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the validation.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` \u2014 GET `/residency/compliance-report`\n\nReturn the current compliance report: every infrastructure component with its EU verification status, including unrecognised types flagged unverified, and the overall percent compliant (US-001-04, NFR-001, NFR-004). Storage of report data is open (GAP-BE-305).\n\n**Request:** \n- `refresh` (boolean): When true, run a fresh scan before building the report so it is current at the launch gate check.\n**Response:** \n- `generated_at` (string, required): Timestamp the report was generated.\n- `percent_compliant` (number, required): Share of infrastructure components verified as EU-hosted.\n- `resources` (array, required): Each component: resource_id, resource_type, region, compliance_status (pass, fail or unverified) and last_scanned_at.\n- `all_compliant` (boolean, required): True only when every component passes.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` \u2014 GET `/residency/drift-alerts`\n\nList drift alerts raised for running resources found outside an EU region, with resource details (AC-001-06-02). Alert channel and recipients are open (GAP-BE-308).\n\n**Request:** \n- `since` (string): Return only alerts raised at or after this timestamp.\n- `page` (integer): Page number. Default and maximum page size are not stated (GAP-BE-500).\n- `limit` (integer): Page size.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Alerts: id, resource_id, resource_type, detected_region and raised_at.\n- `total` (integer, required): Total number of matching alerts.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` \u2014 GET `/residency/launch-gate`\n\nReturn the launch gate status derived from the compliance report (US-001-05, BR-003). Whether a pass authorises release or needs Engineering Lead sign-off is unresolved (CON-BE-151, Q-BE-152).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `gate_status` (string, required): Gate outcome: passed or failed, based on the compliance report.\n- `report_generated_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the compliance report the status is based on.\n- `failing_resources` (array, required): Resources that are non-compliant or unverified and cause a failed gate; empty when passed.\n- `signoff` (object): Engineering Lead sign-off record (approver and time) when one has been recorded. Presence rules are blocked on Q-BE-152.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-548093` \u2014 POST `/residency/launch-gate/signoff`\n\nRecord the Engineering Lead sign-off authorising release (BR-003). Refused while the gate has failed. Blocked on CON-BE-151 and Q-BE-152.\n\n**Request:** \n- `decision` (string, required): approve or reject.\n- `comment` (string): Free-text rationale recorded with the decision.\n**Response:** \n- `gate_status` (string, required): Gate outcome after the decision.\n- `decided_by` (string, required): Identity of the approver.\n- `decided_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the decision.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596` \u2014 POST `/residency/region-checks`\n\nCheck that the supplied infrastructure resources (database, compute, backup storage) are in approved EU regions; fails closed when region metadata is missing or unreadable (US-001-01, US-001-03, BR-001, BR-002, INV-BE-007). Pipeline step may call it; approved region list is open (Q-BE-151).\n\n**Request:** \n- `resources` (array, required): Resources to check; each item carries resource_id, resource_type (database, compute or backup storage) and the region metadata as read from the target.\n- `deployment_ref` (string): Identifier of the deployment or pipeline run being gated, for the audit log.\n**Response:** \n- `result` (string, required): Overall outcome: pass or fail. Fail when any resource is outside an approved EU region or has missing or unreadable region metadata.\n- `deployment_blocked` (boolean, required): True when the result is fail and the deployment must be blocked.\n- `resources` (array, required): Per-resource outcome: resource_id, resource_type, region, compliance_status (pass, fail or unverified) and reason.\n- `checked_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the check.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` \u2014 POST `/residency/scans`\n\nTrigger a residency drift scan of running resources; every scan result is logged and any resource outside an EU region raises a drift alert within 15 minutes of detection (US-001-06, BR-004, NFR-003). The periodic scan itself is a scheduled job, this is the on-demand trigger.\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_ids` (array): Limit the scan to these resources; all resources when omitted.\n**Response:** \n- `scan_id` (string, required): Identifier of the accepted scan.\n- `status` (string, required): Scan state at acceptance, for example queued.\n- `accepted_at` (string, required): Timestamp the scan was accepted.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 5. Invariants\n\nThese hold after every operation of this module.\n\n- `INV-BE-162`. No resource is stored or reported `pass` unless its region is in `approved_regions`. A missing, unreadable or unknown region is never `pass`. This extends INV-BE-007. [AC-001-03-03; BR-001]\n- `INV-BE-163`. A resource of an unrecognised type is present in the report as `unverified`, is never omitted, and is never counted as `pass`. [AC-001-04-02]\n- `INV-BE-164`. Percent compliant equals the number of `pass` resources divided by all resources, times 100, and equals 100 only when at least one resource exists and every resource is `pass` (`ASM-BE-658`). [NFR-001; BR-003]\n- `INV-BE-165`. The gate is `PASSED` only when the latest scan run is `COMPLETED` and `INV-BE-164` gives 100. A sign-off row exists only for a gate that was `PASSED` at the time (`CHECK` in the data model). [BR-003; AC-001-05-02] The sign-off row is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-151`.\n- `INV-BE-166`. A backup validation never writes backup data. A rejected validation leaves no row in any table of this module. [AC-001-02-02]\n- `INV-BE-167`. Every scan run, including one with no findings, ends with a logged result, and every resource found outside an approved region has an alert that is `OPEN`, unless it already had one. [AC-001-06-01, AC-001-06-02; BR-004]\n- `INV-BE-168`. At most one `OPEN` alert exists per resource, and at most one scan run is `RUNNING` (`ASM-BE-662`, `ASM-BE-663`).\n- `INV-BE-169`. No column, log entry, response or alert in this module contains a credential, secret or token. [core rule 12]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Authorization\n\nNo input defines roles or a permission model, and the credential form is `Q-BE-012` (`GAP-BE-085`). A role has no permission on an operation unless an input grants it (`ASM-BE-302`). \"Allow\" below is anchored in the actor named by the cited story; where the link is only implied, it is marked derived.\n\n| Operation | Allowed role | Basis |\n|---|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596` (region check) | Release/CI Pipeline Owner; Platform/DevOps Engineer | US-001-03; US-001-01 |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124` (backup validation) | Platform/DevOps Engineer | US-001-02 |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` (report) | Compliance/Security Auditor; Engineering Lead (derived: US-001-05 reviews the report) | US-001-04; US-001-05 |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` (gate status) | Engineering Lead | US-001-05 |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-548093` (sign-off) | Engineering Lead | BR-003. `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152` |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` (on-demand scan) | Not stated, so denied under `ASM-BE-302`. The actor is not named in US-001-06, which says only that the Platform/DevOps Engineer receives alerts | `GAP-BE-665` does not cover this; it is covered by `GAP-BE-085` |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` (alert list) | Platform/DevOps Engineer | US-001-06; AC-001-06-02 |\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None. These resources describe the system's own infrastructure and are not user-scoped.\n\n**Pipeline caller.** How a CI pipeline authenticates to `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596` is not stated, and it is covered by `GAP-BE-085`.\n\n**Disclosure.** Responses carry infrastructure details but never credentials, secrets or tokens (`INV-BE-169`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Jobs\n\n### 7.1 Periodic drift scan\n\n- **Purpose.** After launch, enumerate running notes_api resources, update `infra_resources`, and raise or close drift alerts. [US-001-06; BR-004]\n- **Period.** No longer than 15 minutes (`ASM-BE-153`, `Q-BE-153`). NFR-003 bounds the alert at 15 minutes from detection. The delay from a resource drifting to the scan detecting it is bounded only by the period, so the worst case from drift to alert is the period plus 15 minutes.\n- **Resource source.** How running resources are enumerated, and with which permissions, is `GAP-BE-664`.\n- **Result.** Every run is logged, with its pass included. [AC-001-06-01]\n- **Single-flight.** At most one run is `RUNNING`. A schedule tick that finds one is skipped (`ASM-BE-663`).\n- **Retry.** A `FAILED` run is not retried inline. The next scheduled run is the retry (`ASM-BE-663`).\n\n### 7.2 Alert delivery\n\nAlerts are sent to the DevOps team with resource details. The channel, recipients and delivery retry are `GAP-BE-308`. `delivered_at` stays null until a delivery is confirmed. That part is `BLOCKED` on `GAP-BE-308`.\n\n### 7.3 Retention sweeps\n\nNone can be specified. Retention for scan runs, alerts, resources and sign-offs is `GAP-BE-666`, and NFR-019 names none of them.\n\n### 7.4 Idempotency\n\n`POST` operations carry no idempotency key and each call is independent, except the single-flight rule for scans (`ASM-BE-665`, `GAP-BE-668`). `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-117596` and `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-174124` are read-only checks and are naturally repeatable (`ASM-BE-660`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Test plan\n\nEvery test is `(planned)`. Time-dependent tests use an injectable clock and a fake resource inventory.\n\n**Unit**\n\n- `test-residency-check-all-eu-passes`, `test-residency-check-non-eu-fails`, `test-residency-check-missing-region-fails`: region classification against `approved_regions`, including an empty table.\n- `test-residency-check-empty-approved-list-fails-all`: an empty list fails every resource (`ASM-BE-655`).\n- `test-percent-compliant-math`, `test-gate-failed-no-evidence`: `INV-BE-164`, `INV-BE-165`, zero resources.\n- `test-report-unrecognised-type-unverified`: `INV-BE-163`.\n- `test-backup-validation-under-30s`: the 30-second bound.\n- `test-alert-state-transitions`: `OPEN` to `CLOSED`, with recurrence creating a new row.\n\n**Integration**\n\n- `test-scan-all-eu-logs-pass-no-alert`, `test-scan-drift-raises-alert`, `test-scan-drift-no-duplicate-open-alert`, `test-scan-drift-alert-within-15-min`.\n- `test-scan-single-flight`: the unique partial index and the refusal.\n- `test-scan-failed-run-gate-failed`: a `FAILED` latest run makes the gate `FAILED`.\n- `test-residency-check-persists-nothing`, `test-backup-validation-writes-nothing`: `INV-BE-166`.\n- `test-signoff-refused-while-failed`. The success path waits on `CON-BE-151`.\n- `test-no-secret-in-logs-or-rows`: `INV-BE-169`.\n\n**Contract**\n\n- Each of the seven operations is present in the OpenAPI document and in the route registry, with its statuses documented. [BR-012; NFR-008]\n- A failing-verdict response and a refused-operation response match the documented error envelope. Statuses wait on `GAP-BE-665`.\n- None of the seven operations matches an excluded scope category. [NFR-010; BR-014 to BR-016]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 9. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\nThis module mints no `CON-` and no `Q-`. Items cited from the project spec are defined once in its open-item register and only referenced here.\n\n**Cited from the project spec**\n\n- **Blocking.** `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152` block the sign-off and the release-authority reading of the gate.\n- **Open inputs.** `Q-BE-151` (approved region list), `Q-BE-153` and `ASM-BE-153` (scan period), `GAP-BE-305` (storage), `GAP-BE-307` (backup frequency and retention), `GAP-BE-308` (alert channel).\n- **Cross-cutting.** `GAP-BE-085`, `ASM-BE-302`, `Q-BE-012` (roles and credentials), `GAP-BE-304` (encryption), `GAP-BE-500` (page size), `ASM-BE-301` (proposed error code names), `ASM-BE-019` (email is personal data, applied to `signed_by`).\n\n**Gaps minted by this module**\n\n| ID | Statement | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-664` | How running resources are enumerated for a scan, and with what permissions, is not stated. | A platform decision on the discovery mechanism. |\n| `GAP-BE-665` | Statuses and error codes for a failing region-check verdict, a rejected backup validation, a refused scan trigger and a refused sign-off are not stated. | A status decision, which DevAgent renders into the contract. |\n| `GAP-BE-666` | Retention of `infra_resources`, `scan_runs`, `drift_alerts` and sign-offs is not stated. | A retention decision. |\n| `GAP-BE-667` | No maximum age is stated for the scan results a gate evaluation may rely on, although NFR-004 requires the report refreshed at each gate check. | A freshness rule from the product owner. |\n| `GAP-BE-668` | No idempotency requirement is stated for the scan trigger, the sign-off, or the two checks. | A contract decision. |\n| `GAP-BE-669` | Whether a resource whose region cannot be read in a post-launch scan raises a drift alert is not stated. BR-004 speaks only of a resource detected outside an EU region. | A rule from the product owner. |\n\n**Assumptions minted by this module**\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-653` | Residency records live in the notes_api PostgreSQL database in an EU region. | No other store is named, and BR-001 requires EU placement. | `GAP-BE-305` |\n| `ASM-BE-654` | Types are `uuid` ids generated by the service, `text`, `timestamptz`, `integer` and `numeric(5,2)`. | PostgreSQL 16 is the platform and no types are supplied. | Platform decision |\n| `ASM-BE-655` | An approved region is exactly a row of `approved_regions`. The table is empty until the list is supplied, and an empty list makes every resource `fail`. No region name is assumed. | AC-001-01-01 refers to an approved list that no input contains. Failing closed matches AC-001-03-03. | `Q-BE-151` |\n| `ASM-BE-656` | The status vocabulary is `pass`, `fail` and `unverified`. | AC-001-04-01 and AC-001-04-02 name pass/fail and `unverified`. | Product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-657` | Recognised resource types are `database`, `compute` and `backup-storage`. Any other type is `unverified`. | BR-001 names database, compute and backups. The names are labels. | Product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-658` | Percent compliant counts `pass` over all resources, with `fail` and `unverified` in the denominator, to two decimals. Zero resources gives 0 and a `FAILED` gate. | BR-003 requires 100% EU-verified, and AC-001-04-02 forbids omitting unverified resources. No evidence is not 100%. | Product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-659` | The gate is computed at request time from persisted results of the latest scan run, which must be `COMPLETED`. A gate request triggers no scan. | NFR-004 requires an accurate report at each gate check; no input says a gate request triggers a scan. | `GAP-BE-667` |\n| `ASM-BE-660` | The region check and the backup validation are stateless and persist nothing except a log entry. | US-001-04 ties report data to scans, and no input says a pipeline check updates the records. | Product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-661` | A resource whose region cannot be read in a scan is recorded `fail` and raises no alert. | It extends AC-001-03-03 to scans. BR-004 speaks only of resources detected outside the EU. | `GAP-BE-669` |\n| `ASM-BE-662` | At most one drift alert is `OPEN` per resource. An alert is `CLOSED` when a later scan reads the resource in an approved region or finds it gone, and a recurrence creates a new alert. | BR-004 requires an alert per detection and no input defines acknowledgement. | Product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-663` | At most one scan run is `RUNNING`. A trigger or tick that finds one is refused or skipped. A `FAILED` run is not retried inline, and the next scheduled run is the retry. | No input states concurrency or retry for scans. | Operations decision |\n| `ASM-BE-664` | The 30 seconds in AC-001-02-02 run from receipt of the validation request to the response, and the validation inspects the configuration only. | The story speaks of the backup pipeline validating the config before running. | Contract decision |\n| `ASM-BE-665` | None of the seven operations takes an idempotency key. | No input states one. | `GAP-BE-668` |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-001-01", "US-001-02", "US-001-03", "US-001-04", "US-001-05", "US-001-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/api-docs-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/api-docs-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: api-docs\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [GAP-BE-303, GAP-BE-309, Q-BE-012, CON-BE-007]\nscope: [API-APIDOCS-304307, API-APIDOCS-827943, API-APIDOCS-639507, API-APIDOCS-057616]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** `api-docs` publishes the machine-readable contract of notes_api and the facts an integrator needs to use it. [backend.module-map; stories.md EP-004] It owns:\n\n1. **The OpenAPI document.** A valid OpenAPI 3.x document listing every implemented endpoint, with request and response schemas, optional-parameter defaults and documented 4xx and 5xx errors. Operation `API-APIDOCS-304307`. [stories.md US-004-01, US-004-02, US-004-03; requirements.md BR-012, NFR-008]\n2. **The coverage metric.** The percentage of implemented endpoints present in the published document, naming any that are missing. Operation `API-APIDOCS-827943`. [stories.md US-004-04]\n3. **The data-handling note.** It states where note data is stored and served, and is also carried in the OpenAPI document. Operation `API-APIDOCS-639507`. [stories.md US-004-06; requirements.md BR-013]\n4. **Example payloads.** Requests and responses for search and tagging, including an empty search result. Operation `API-APIDOCS-057616`. [stories.md US-004-05; requirements.md NFR-009]\n5. **Interface-change documentation.** The `pinned` field and pinned-first ordering, and the email one-time-code send and verify endpoints, are documented as interface changes. [requirements.md NFR-014, NFR-015, NFR-016]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- The content of the schemas and the error envelope. They are defined once, in `contracts/schemas/` and in `backend.error-model`, and are referenced here (core rule 6).\n- The behaviour of the documented endpoints. Each owning module (`auth`, `notes`, `search`, `residency-compliance`, `scope-guardrail`) owns its behaviour.\n- The Scope section, its approval workflow and its docs-build lint. They belong to `scope-guardrail`. [stories.md US-005-01]\n- The scope-guardrail contract test suite, which consumes this module's document. [backend.module-map; stories.md US-005-05]\n- Residency verification itself. The data-handling note only reports the outcome. Verification belongs to `residency-compliance`.\n- Publishing, versioning or tagging the contract package. The orchestrator owns that.\n- Rendering `API-` ids. DevAgent mints them.\n\n**Operations owned.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`, `API-APIDOCS-827943`, `API-APIDOCS-639507`, `API-APIDOCS-057616`, referenced by id only.\n\n**Reading used.** \"All endpoints\" in US-004-01 means every implemented endpoint (`ASM-BE-704`). The served document is built from the machine-readable contract artefact (`ASM-BE-710`).\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-703` onward, `GAP-BE-714` onward, `INV-BE-212` onward, `Q-BE-501` onward. It mints no `CON-`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.data-model | status: DERIVED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**This module owns no persistent data and has no tables, columns, indexes or migrations.** [backend.module-map: \"Owns data: none\"; backend.migration-plan lists no `api-docs` migration]\n\nIt reads three inputs, owned elsewhere:\n\n| Input | Owner | Use here |\n|---|---|---|\n| The published OpenAPI document | built from the contract artefact (`ASM-BE-710`, `GAP-BE-715`) | served by `API-APIDOCS-304307`; parsed by the coverage check |\n| The route registry | the application | the set of implemented endpoints [requirements.md BR-012: \"OpenAPI spec vs. route registry\"] |\n| The schemas in `contracts/schemas/` | backend, once | referenced from the document, never restated |\n\n**Computed values (not stored).** A coverage evaluation yields:\n\n- the count of implemented endpoints;\n- the count documented;\n- a percentage;\n- the list of undocumented endpoints.\n\nThe response shape is a contract matter, rendered by DevAgent, and its definition is `GAP-BE-716`. Counting rules are `ASM-BE-706`.\n\n**Time and PII.** No timestamps are stored. No PII is held. Examples and the data-handling note contain none (`INV-BE-217`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nThe module has no stored entity. Two derived lifecycles carry the rules.\n\n### 3.1 Endpoint documentation lifecycle\n\nStates: `UNREGISTERED`, `DOCUMENTED`, `CALLABLE`. `CALLABLE` is not terminal while the endpoint exists.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `UNREGISTERED` | `DOCUMENTED` | spec regenerated and published with the endpoint | entry has schemas and errors | endpoint appears in the document |\n| `DOCUMENTED` | `CALLABLE` | endpoint enabled in the route registry | already `DOCUMENTED`, or documented at the same moment | endpoint accepts requests |\n| `UNREGISTERED` | `CALLABLE` | endpoint enabled with no document entry | **illegal** | coverage is below 100% and names the endpoint (`INV-BE-212`, `INV-BE-214`); the build outcome is `ASM-BE-711` |\n\n[stories.md US-004-01, US-004-04]\n\n### 3.2 Coverage evaluation (per run, nothing persisted)\n\nStates: `REQUESTED`, `PARSE_FAILED` (terminal), `COMPLETE` (terminal), `INCOMPLETE` (terminal).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `REQUESTED` | `PARSE_FAILED` | check runs | the document is malformed | fails with a clear parsing error; no coverage figure is reported |\n| `REQUESTED` | `COMPLETE` | check runs | document parses and every implemented endpoint is documented | reports 100% |\n| `REQUESTED` | `INCOMPLETE` | check runs | document parses and at least one implemented endpoint is undocumented | reports below 100% and names each missing endpoint |\n\n[stories.md US-004-04] The three results are terminal for that evaluation. The next evaluation starts again at `REQUESTED`.\n\n**Illegal transitions on the four operations.** All four are reads and have no illegal transitions. Their failure statuses are `GAP-BE-717`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-001 Serve the OpenAPI document\n**Given** notes_api is deployed and the caller is permitted (`ASM-BE-705`),\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-304307` is requested,\n**Then** a valid OpenAPI 3.x document in JSON or YAML is returned, listing all CRUD, tagging, search, archive and email one-time-code login (send and verify) endpoints.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01; BR-012; NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-openapi-valid-and-complete` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** The path is a proposal (`GAP-BE-303`). The format is `GAP-BE-719`. The verify-response content is blocked on `Q-BE-012`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-002 Publish before callable\n**Given** a new endpoint is added to notes_api,\n**When** the spec is regenerated and published,\n**Then** the endpoint appears in the document before, or at the same time as, it becomes callable.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01; BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-endpoint-documented-before-callable` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** Mechanism is `GAP-BE-715`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-003 Unsupported Accept header\n**Given** a request with an unsupported `Accept` header,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-304307` is requested,\n**Then** the default-format document is returned with an explicit content type naming that format (`ASM-BE-703`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (alternative: 406).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-accept-fallback` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. The 406 option is open in `GAP-BE-309`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-004 Schemas documented\n**Given** an endpoint accepts a note payload,\n**When** its schema is inspected in the document,\n**Then** all required fields, types and constraints are documented, by reference to `contracts/schemas/`, for notes, tags, search and archive.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02; BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-schemas-present-and-referenced` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** Constraints such as title length and tag format are not stated in any input (`GAP-BE-035`).\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-005 Optional parameters and defaults\n**Given** an endpoint supports optional query parameters, such as search filters,\n**When** its schema is reviewed,\n**Then** each optional parameter is marked optional and its default is documented. The cited default is `include_archived` excluding archived notes (BR-009). Page size defaults are `GAP-BE-500`.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02; BR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-optional-params-defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-006 Type drift fails CI\n**Given** a documented field type is changed in code and the document is not updated,\n**When** the spec-validation check runs in CI,\n**Then** the check fails and flags the mismatch.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-type-drift-fails-ci` (planned).\n- **API.** none (CI check).\n- **Status.** Tooling is not named (`GAP-BE-715`, `GAP-BE-089`).\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-007 Validation errors documented\n**Given** an endpoint can return validation errors,\n**When** the document is reviewed,\n**Then** a 400 response schema with the error structure and fields is documented, using the envelope in `backend.error-model` (`ASM-BE-300`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03; BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-400-documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-008 Authentication errors documented\n**Given** an endpoint requires authentication or authorisation,\n**When** the document is reviewed,\n**Then** 401 and 403 responses are documented with the expected body shape.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-401-403-documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-009 Login-endpoint errors documented\n**Given** the email one-time-code endpoints POST `/auth/otp/send` and POST `/auth/otp/verify`,\n**When** the document is reviewed,\n**Then** the send endpoint documents a 415 response for more than 3 requests within 10 minutes, and the verify endpoint documents a locked-account response after 5 wrong codes, locked for 15 minutes. The behaviour is owned by `auth` and referenced, not restated.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03; BR-028; BR-032.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-login-errors-documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** The locked-account status is `GAP-BE-038`. The wrong, expired and used-code statuses are `GAP-BE-053`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-010 Documented 404 matches behaviour\n**Given** an endpoint documents a 404 response,\n**When** the documented 404 is checked against actual API behaviour,\n**Then** they match (`ASM-BE-708`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-404-matches-behaviour` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** The attachment-path 404 against the structured scope error is `CON-BE-007`, so the documented response for that path is blocked on its resolution.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-011 Coverage at 100%\n**Given** all implemented endpoints are documented,\n**When** the coverage check runs or `API-APIDOCS-827943` is requested,\n**Then** it reports 100% coverage.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04; NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-100` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n- **Status.** The counting unit and formula are `ASM-BE-706` and `GAP-BE-716`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-012 Coverage below 100%\n**Given** one or more implemented endpoints are missing from the document,\n**When** the coverage check runs,\n**Then** it reports less than 100% and names every missing endpoint.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-names-missing` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n- **Status.** Whether this fails the build is `ASM-BE-711` and `Q-BE-502`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-013 Malformed document\n**Given** the spec file is malformed,\n**When** the coverage check runs,\n**Then** it fails with a clear parsing error and reports no coverage figure.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-malformed-spec` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n- **Status.** The failure status is `GAP-BE-717`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-014 Search examples\n**Given** the search endpoint is documented,\n**When** a developer views the document or requests `API-APIDOCS-057616`,\n**Then** an example request with query parameters and an example response body are shown, and an example empty-result response is included.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-05; NFR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-search-examples` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-057616`, `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** Response structure is `GAP-BE-721`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-015 Tagging examples and full example coverage\n**Given** the tagging operations (add and remove a tag),\n**When** the document is reviewed,\n**Then** every search and tagging endpoint has at least one example payload.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-009; US-004-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-examples-100pct` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-057616`.\n- **Status.** The tag operation paths are `GAP-BE-303`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-016 Examples are masked or synthetic\n**Given** any example payload,\n**When** it is written or served,\n**Then** it contains only masked or synthetic data.\n- **Traces to.** Core section 12; the `API-APIDOCS-057616` operation record.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-examples-no-pii` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-057616`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-017 Data-handling note\n**Given** an operations engineer reviews the documentation,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-639507` is requested or the document is read,\n**Then** it states that all note data is stored and served within the EU.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06; BR-013; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-data-handling-note` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-639507`, `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** The approved EU region list is not supplied (`Q-BE-151`), so the claim cannot be verified (`GAP-BE-720`).\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-018 Archived versus active storage\n**Given** archive endpoints could behave differently from active-note endpoints regarding storage location,\n**When** the documentation is reviewed,\n**Then** any difference is explicitly called out. Under `ASM-BE-709` there is none, and the note says archived and active notes share one store.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06; BR-007; BR-013.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-archive-storage-note` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-639507`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-019 Pinned interface change\n**Given** the `pinned` field and the pinned-first ordering of list and search results,\n**When** the document is published,\n**Then** both are documented as an interface change. The ordering rule is owned by `search` and referenced (`INV-BE-001`).\n- **Traces to.** NFR-014; BR-018; BR-019.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-pinned-documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-020 Login interface change\n**Given** the email one-time-code endpoints,\n**When** the document is published,\n**Then** both are documented as an additive change, existing login methods are stated as unchanged, and consumers are notified that they must adapt.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-015; NFR-016; BR-032.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-otp-additive-change` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-304307`.\n- **Status.** The notification channel is `GAP-BE-718`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-APIDOCS-304307` | GET | `/openapi` | `api-docs` | 200 | 406, 500 | Return the published OpenAPI document describing every notes_api endpoint (US-004-01, US-004-02). Path is a proposal pending GAP-BE-303; behaviour for an unsupported Accept header (406 versus default-format fallback) is open per GAP-BE-309. |\n| `API-APIDOCS-827943` | GET | `/openapi/coverage` | `api-docs` | 200 | 401, 403, 500 | Return the endpoint coverage metric: percentage of implemented endpoints present in the published spec, naming any missing endpoints (US-004-04, NFR-008). |\n| `API-APIDOCS-639507` | GET | `/openapi/data-handling` | `api-docs` | 200 | 500 | Return the data-handling note stating that all note data is stored and served within the EU (US-004-06, BR-013). |\n| `API-APIDOCS-057616` | GET | `/openapi/examples` | `api-docs` | 200 | 400, 404, 500 | Return example request and response payloads for the search and tagging endpoints (US-004-05, NFR-009). Examples use masked or synthetic data only. |\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-304307` \u2014 GET `/openapi`\n\nReturn the published OpenAPI document describing every notes_api endpoint (US-004-01, US-004-02). Path is a proposal pending GAP-BE-303; behaviour for an unsupported Accept header (406 versus default-format fallback) is open per GAP-BE-309.\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `openapi` (string, required): OpenAPI specification version declared by the document.\n- `info` (object, required): API title and document version metadata.\n- `paths` (object, required): Every endpoint with its methods, parameters, request and response schemas and documented error responses.\n- `components` (object): Shared schemas, including the error envelope, and security schemes.\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-827943` \u2014 GET `/openapi/coverage`\n\nReturn the endpoint coverage metric: percentage of implemented endpoints present in the published spec, naming any missing endpoints (US-004-04, NFR-008).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `coverage_percent` (number, required): Percentage of implemented endpoints documented in the published OpenAPI document.\n- `total_endpoints` (integer, required): Number of endpoints registered in the route registry.\n- `documented_endpoints` (integer, required): Number of those endpoints present in the OpenAPI document.\n- `missing_endpoints` (array, required): Endpoints (method and path) that are implemented but absent from the document; empty when coverage is 100%.\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-639507` \u2014 GET `/openapi/data-handling`\n\nReturn the data-handling note stating that all note data is stored and served within the EU (US-004-06, BR-013).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `statement` (string, required): Data-residency statement: all note data is stored and served within the EU.\n- `scope` (array): Data categories the statement covers, such as database, compute and backups.\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-057616` \u2014 GET `/openapi/examples`\n\nReturn example request and response payloads for the search and tagging endpoints (US-004-05, NFR-009). Examples use masked or synthetic data only.\n\n**Request:** \n- `endpoint` (string): Restrict the result to examples for a single documented endpoint (method and path).\n**Response:** \n- `examples` (array, required): List of examples, each naming its endpoint with a request payload and a response payload.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-212`. No implemented endpoint is callable while absent from the published document. [stories.md US-004-01; requirements.md BR-012]\n- `INV-BE-213`. The coverage metric reports 100% only if the document parses and every implemented endpoint is documented. A malformed document yields a parsing error, never a coverage figure. [stories.md US-004-04]\n- `INV-BE-214`. Whenever coverage is below 100%, every missing endpoint is named. [stories.md US-004-04]\n- `INV-BE-215`. Every documented field type and constraint equals the implementation. A mismatch fails the CI check. [stories.md US-004-02]\n- `INV-BE-216`. Every documented 404 response matches the actual 404 behaviour (`ASM-BE-708`). [stories.md US-004-03]\n- `INV-BE-217`. No example, data-handling note or other document body contains a credential, a real-looking email address, a real-looking one-time code or other PII. [core section 12; `API-APIDOCS-057616` operation record]\n- `INV-BE-218`. Every search endpoint and every tagging endpoint has at least one example, and search has an empty-result example. [requirements.md NFR-009; stories.md US-004-05]\n- `INV-BE-219`. None of the four operations changes any state, because all four are GET. [operation records]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\nNo input states who may fetch the document, the coverage metric, the data-handling note or the examples. The project auth model records this as \"not stated\" (`backend.auth-model`, `GAP-BE-303`). A role has no permission unless an input grants it (`ASM-BE-302`). Default applied: the four operations require an authenticated caller (`ASM-BE-705`, question `Q-BE-501`, gap `GAP-BE-714`).\n\n| Operation | Unauthenticated caller | Authenticated team member | Other actors |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-APIDOCS-304307` | Deny (default) | Allow under `ASM-BE-705` | Integration developer [US-004-01]: not stated |\n| `API-APIDOCS-827943` | Deny (default) | Allow under `ASM-BE-705` | Backend developer [US-004-04]: not stated |\n| `API-APIDOCS-639507` | Deny (default) | Allow under `ASM-BE-705` | Operations engineer [US-004-06]: not stated |\n| `API-APIDOCS-057616` | Deny (default) | Allow under `ASM-BE-705` | Integration developer [US-004-05]: not stated |\n\n**Consequence of the default.** An integrator without a credential cannot read the document. That is why the default is a question, not a fact. The credential form is `Q-BE-012` and eligibility is `Q-BE-003` (`CON-BE-003`).\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None. The resources are global and not user-scoped.\n\n**Content constraint.** The document, note and examples are read-only artefacts and expose no PII (`INV-BE-217`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n| Job | Trigger | Does | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Spec publish | an endpoint is added or changed | regenerates and publishes the document before or with the endpoint becoming callable | US-004-01 (`BE-API-DOCS-FR-002`); mechanism `GAP-BE-715` |\n| Spec validation | CI | parses the document; compares documented types with the code; fails on mismatch | US-004-02 (`BE-API-DOCS-FR-006`); tooling `GAP-BE-715` |\n| Coverage check | CI, and on request via `API-APIDOCS-827943` | route registry versus document; reports percentage and missing endpoints; fails with a parsing error on a malformed document | US-004-04; build outcome `ASM-BE-711` |\n| Example validation | CI | validates each example against its documented schema | `ASM-BE-707` |\n\n**Idempotency and retries.** All four operations are GET and idempotent, and no idempotency key applies. No retry policy is stated for the CI jobs.\n\n**Scope lint.** The docs-build lint for a missing Scope section is owned by `scope-guardrail`. [stories.md US-005-01]\n\n**Rate-limit class.** Not stated (`GAP-BE-717`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\nAll tests are planned. Test data uses synthetic values only (`INV-BE-217`).\n\n**Unit**\n\n- `test-apidocs-coverage-100`, `test-apidocs-coverage-names-missing`, `test-apidocs-coverage-malformed-spec`. These use a fixture registry and fixture documents, including an empty registry (`GAP-BE-716`).\n- `test-apidocs-accept-fallback`.\n- `test-apidocs-type-drift-fails-ci`.\n- `test-apidocs-examples-no-pii`.\n\n**Integration**\n\n- `test-apidocs-openapi-valid-and-complete`: the served document parses as OpenAPI 3.x and lists the note, tag, search, archive and login endpoints.\n- `test-apidocs-endpoint-documented-before-callable`.\n- `test-apidocs-schemas-present-and-referenced` and `test-apidocs-optional-params-defaults`.\n- `test-apidocs-400-documented`, `test-apidocs-401-403-documented`, `test-apidocs-login-errors-documented`.\n- `test-apidocs-data-handling-note` and `test-apidocs-archive-storage-note`.\n- `test-apidocs-search-examples`, `test-apidocs-examples-100pct`.\n- `test-apidocs-pinned-documented` and `test-apidocs-otp-additive-change`.\n\n**Contract**\n\n- `test-apidocs-404-matches-behaviour`: each documented 404 equals the actual response.\n- `test-apidocs-registry-vs-document`: every route in the registry has a document entry (`INV-BE-212`).\n- `test-apidocs-examples-match-schemas`: examples validate against their schemas (`ASM-BE-707`).\n- Each of the four operations is compared with its rendered contract entry.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions, assumptions\n\n**Contradiction check, all four directions.**\n\n- Story to design: the design brief holds no acceptance criteria for US-004-01 to US-004-06 (`GAP-BE-722`). No contradiction.\n- Story to requirement: none new. US-004-01 lists specific endpoint families and BR-012 requires every endpoint (`ASM-BE-704`). Not a conflict.\n- Requirement to requirement: none new. The attachment-path conflict affects what is documented and is `CON-BE-007`. NFR-014, NFR-015 and NFR-016 are consistent.\n- Design to design: none.\n\nNo `CON-` is minted by this module. Cited: `CON-BE-007`, `CON-BE-003`, `CON-BE-006`.\n\n### Questions\n\n| ID | Class | Question | Default if unanswered |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-501` | degradable | Who may call the four operations? Must an integrator authenticate? | Authenticated caller required (`ASM-BE-705`) |\n| `Q-BE-502` | degradable | Does coverage below 100% fail the CI build, or only report? | Fails the build (`ASM-BE-711`) |\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | Missing | Degrades | Done instead | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-714` | Access rule for the four operations | authorization | default `ASM-BE-705` | answer to `Q-BE-501` |\n| `GAP-BE-715` | How the document is produced, stored, kept in sync, and the CI tooling (no library named, `GAP-BE-089`) | FR-002, FR-006, jobs | built from the contract artefact (`ASM-BE-710`) | a platform and operations decision |\n| `GAP-BE-716` | Definition of the coverage metric: denominator, counting unit, rounding, empty-registry case, response shape | FR-011 to FR-013 | `ASM-BE-706` | a metric definition from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-717` | Failure statuses of the four operations (including malformed-spec failure) and their rate-limit class | contract | none written | a status and rate-limit decision |\n| `GAP-BE-718` | How consumers are notified that they must adapt [NFR-014, NFR-015, NFR-016] | FR-019, FR-020 | document the change only | a notification-channel decision |\n| `GAP-BE-719` | Format of the document, data-handling note and examples (JSON or YAML, media types) | FR-001, FR-003 | JSON default fallback (`ASM-BE-703`) | a format decision |\n| `GAP-BE-720` | The EU claim cannot be verified: approved region list absent (`Q-BE-151`); no input says whether publication depends on a passing compliance report; exact wording not given | FR-017 | restate US-004-06 wording only | region list and a publication rule |\n| `GAP-BE-721` | Response structure of `API-APIDOCS-057616`; which endpoints count as tagging (paths `GAP-BE-303`); whether examples also live inside the document | FR-014, FR-015 | none | contract decision and path decisions |\n| `GAP-BE-722` | Design brief has no acceptance criteria for US-004-01 to US-004-06; inline story criteria used | whole module | stories used as sole source | acceptance-criteria files for EP-004 |\n\nAlso cited and not redefined: `GAP-BE-303`, `GAP-BE-309`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-038`, `GAP-BE-053`, `GAP-BE-089`, `GAP-BE-500`, `Q-BE-012`, `Q-BE-003`, `Q-BE-151`.\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-703` | An unsupported `Accept` header returns the default-format (JSON) document with an explicit content type, not 406. | US-004-01 permits either; a fallback still serves the integrator. | decision on `GAP-BE-309` |\n| `ASM-BE-704` | \"All endpoints\" in US-004-01 means every implemented endpoint, including these four and any later-pathed endpoints. | BR-012 says \"every implemented endpoint\". | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-705` | The four operations require an authenticated caller. | `ASM-BE-302`: no grant exists in any input. | `Q-BE-501` |\n| `ASM-BE-706` | The counting unit is an operation, a (method, path) pair. Coverage is documented divided by implemented, against the route registry. | BR-012: \"entry for every endpoint\", checked against the route registry. | `GAP-BE-716` |\n| `ASM-BE-707` | Each example is validated against its schema in CI. | Otherwise examples go stale (core rule 6 rationale). | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-708` | The \"Given\" of US-004-03's third criterion is a misplaced copy of US-004-02's. The intended criterion is that the documented 404 matches actual behaviour. | Its \"When\" checks the 404 against the spec. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-709` | Archived and active notes share one store, so the storage note has no difference to call out. | Archiving is a status flag, not a hard delete [BR-007; backend.domain-model]. | `residency-compliance` and operations |\n| `ASM-BE-710` | The served document is built from `contracts/openapi.yaml`. | It is the machine-readable contract named in the output tree. | `GAP-BE-715` |\n| `ASM-BE-711` | Coverage below 100% fails the CI build. | NFR-008 targets 100% endpoint coverage and BR-012 requires an automated check. | `Q-BE-502` |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-004-01", "US-004-02", "US-004-03", "US-004-04", "US-004-05", "US-004-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/scope-guardrail-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/scope-guardrail-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: scope-guardrail\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: []\nscope: []\n```\n\n`scope:` is empty because DevAgent supplied no operation records for this module. The module owns behaviours (refusals, a documentation section, a CI test suite), not endpoints of its own. [RUN-CONTEXT module operations list is empty; backend.module-map; READINESS \"module(s) defining none: scope-guardrail\"]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The module keeps `notes_api` inside its documented minimal scope. The excluded categories are mobile apps (mobile-specific endpoints), attachments, and real-time collaboration. [stories.md US-005-01; requirements.md BR-014, BR-015, BR-016; backend.module-map]\n\n1. **Scope section.** It defines the content of the published \"Scope\" section, which lists the three exclusions. [US-005-01]\n2. **Scope change control and build lint.** A change to the Scope section needs review and approval before merge, and a docs build with no Scope section fails. [US-005-01]\n3. **No excluded routes exist.** There is no attachment or file-upload route and no real-time channel (WebSocket, SSE, polling-push). [BR-014, BR-015; US-005-02, US-005-03]\n4. **Standard refusal.** A call to an unsupported out-of-scope endpoint returns a clear, documented, structured error with code `OUT_OF_SCOPE`. It is not a silent failure or a bare 404. [BR-016, NFR-011; US-005-04]\n5. **Contract test suite.** A suite runs on every CI build and passes only when zero endpoints match an excluded category. A matching new endpoint fails the build and blocks the merge. [NFR-010; US-005-05]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- **Logging and classifying stakeholder requests.** US-005-06 and BR-017 describe a product-management process, not a backend function. [GAP-BE-001]\n- **The OpenAPI document.** It is generated, published, covered and validated by `api-docs`. This module supplies the Scope content and the `OUT_OF_SCOPE` error definition, and reads the document in its test suite. [backend.module-map]\n- **The error envelope and the full error-code registry.** They are defined once in `backend.error-model`. This module uses `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and defines no other code. [GAP-BE-300, ASM-BE-300]\n- **Note data and note semantics.** The `notes` module owns create, edit and the concurrency choice for PATCH. This module states only the guardrail on that behaviour (BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-009).\n- **Authentication and authorisation.** The `auth` module and `backend.auth-model` own them.\n- **Persistent data.** This module owns none. [backend.module-map]\n\n**Operations owned.** None. No operation record was supplied, so no operation id is referenced.\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints `ASM-BE-753` onward, `GAP-BE-764` onward and `INV-BE-262` onward from its reserved block. It mints no `CON-` and no `Q-`. The unattended run takes the stated default for every degradable point. Other ids are cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.data-model | status: DERIVED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**No tables, columns, indexes or migrations.** The module map states \"Owns data: none\" for `scope-guardrail`. [backend.module-map] No input asks for a persisted record of rejected out-of-scope calls. The rejected-requests KPI refers to stakeholder requests logged by a product manager. [BR-017; US-005-06]\n\nConsequences:\n\n- A refused request writes nothing. [US-005-02: \"persists no file content\"]\n- Refusals are observable through application logs and metrics (`backend.observability`), not through a table. No log field list is supplied. [GAP-BE-501]\n- The Scope section and the exclusion list are documentation artefacts. Where the exclusion list is stored is `GAP-BE-764`.\n- Referential integrity, `ON DELETE` behaviour, time handling and PII columns do not apply.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.state-machine | status: DERIVED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\n**No entity in this module has a status column, so there is no persisted state machine and no terminal state.** The behaviour is stateless per request or per build.\n\n### 3.1 Request decision (per request, no memory)\n\n| Condition | Outcome | Status |\n|---|---|---|\n| Request targets an attachment path (e.g. `POST /notes/{id}/attachments`) | Refused with an `OUT_OF_SCOPE` error body | 404, reading in `ASM-BE-753` [US-005-02] |\n| Multipart/form-data file payload to a note create or update endpoint | Refused with an `OUT_OF_SCOPE` error body; no file content persisted | 415 [US-005-02; US-005-04] |\n| WebSocket upgrade or SSE connection attempt | Connection rejected; no protocol upgrade | \"standard error status\", `GAP-BE-309`, `GAP-BE-765` |\n| Other mobile-specific or real-time call | Refused with a documented 4xx and an explanatory message | `GAP-BE-767` [NFR-011] |\n\nThe mechanism named for producing the refusal is \"API gateway/error-handler\". [BR-016]\n\n### 3.2 Guardrail test result (per CI build)\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| running | PASS | suite finishes | zero endpoints match an excluded category [US-005-05] |\n| running | FAIL | suite finds a match | at least one endpoint matches an excluded pattern (e.g. `/attachments`, `/ws`); the build fails and the merge is blocked [US-005-05] |\n\nEach build starts afresh and nothing carries over. PASS and FAIL are results of one run, not stored states.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-001 Published Scope section\n**Given** the published API documentation,\n**When** a reader views the \"Scope\" section,\n**Then** mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration are listed as explicitly excluded.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (criterion 1); BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-section-lists-exclusions` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The section lives in the documentation `api-docs` publishes (`ASM-BE-755`).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-002 Scope changes need approval\n**Given** a proposal to add or change an out-of-scope item,\n**When** it is submitted for merge,\n**Then** it requires review and approval before it merges.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (criterion 2).\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-change-requires-approval` (planned, process check).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The approver and the enforcing mechanism are `GAP-BE-766`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-003 Docs build fails without a Scope section\n**Given** the docs build pipeline,\n**When** the Scope section is missing from a build,\n**Then** the documentation build fails with a lint or validation error.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (criterion 3).\n- **Verification.** `test-docs-build-fails-without-scope` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The docs build tooling is not named (`GAP-BE-766`, `GAP-BE-089`).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-004 No attachment or file-upload route\n**Given** the deployed route registry,\n**When** its routes are enumerated,\n**Then** none is an attachment or file-upload endpoint.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02; BR-014; NFR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-no-attachment-routes` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The pattern list is `GAP-BE-764`. The registry source is `GAP-BE-770`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-005 Attachment path is refused with a scope message\n**Given** a request to an attachment path (e.g. `POST /notes/{id}/attachments`),\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** the response is 404 and its body is the standard error envelope with code `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a message referencing the scope exclusion.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02 (criterion 1); US-005-04 (criterion 1); BR-016; NFR-011.\n- **Verification.** `test-attachment-path-out-of-scope` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** DRAFT-UNVERIFIED. US-005-02 requires 404 and BR-016 asks for no \"404 ambiguity\". The reading used is `ASM-BE-753`, and the unresolved contradiction is `CON-BE-007`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-006 Multipart file upload is refused\n**Given** a multipart/form-data request with a file payload to a note create or update endpoint,\n**When** the request is validated,\n**Then** the response is 415 with a structured error (code `OUT_OF_SCOPE`, human-readable reason) and no file content is persisted.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02 (criterion 2); US-005-04 (criterion 2).\n- **Verification.** `test-multipart-upload-415-out-of-scope`, `test-multipart-upload-persists-nothing` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The code is `OUT_OF_SCOPE` per `ASM-BE-754`. The registry overlap is `GAP-BE-769`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-007 No real-time channel exists\n**Given** the deployed API,\n**When** its endpoints are enumerated,\n**Then** none is a WebSocket, SSE or polling-push channel for collaboration.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03; BR-015; NFR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-no-realtime-endpoints` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The pattern list is `GAP-BE-764`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-008 WebSocket and SSE attempts are rejected\n**Given** the running API server,\n**When** a client attempts a WebSocket upgrade or an SSE connection to any notes_api endpoint,\n**Then** the connection is rejected, no protocol upgrade occurs, and a standard error status is returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03 (criterion 1); BR-015.\n- **Verification.** `test-websocket-upgrade-rejected`, `test-sse-connection-rejected` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The exact status is `GAP-BE-309`. The detection signal and body are `GAP-BE-765`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-009 Concurrent PATCH has no broadcast or merge\n**Given** two clients submit PATCH requests to the same note concurrently,\n**When** both requests are processed,\n**Then** the API applies a standard last-write or conflict response, with no live broadcast and no merge logic.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03 (criterion 2).\n- **Verification.** `test-concurrent-patch-no-broadcast-no-merge` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The `notes` module owns the choice between last-write and conflict (its `ASM-BE-360`). Nothing here restates it.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-010 Mobile-specific endpoints are absent and refused\n**Given** a request to a mobile-specific endpoint,\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** no such route exists, and the response is a documented 4xx carrying code `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and an explanatory message.\n- **Traces to.** BR-016; NFR-011; US-005-04 (criterion 1); US-005-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-mobile-specific-endpoint-out-of-scope` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` in part: no input defines what makes an endpoint mobile-specific (`GAP-BE-764`), and the status is `GAP-BE-767`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-011 The out-of-scope error is documented\n**Given** the published OpenAPI document,\n**When** the out-of-scope error is looked up,\n**Then** the documented 4xx response and the `OUT_OF_SCOPE` code are present with their explanatory message shape.\n- **Traces to.** BR-016 (\"clear, documented error\"); NFR-011 (\"documented 4xx error\").\n- **Verification.** `test-openapi-documents-out-of-scope-error` (planned).\n- **API.** none. The error definition is referenced in `backend.error-model` and rendered by `api-docs`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-012 Contract suite passes only on zero matches\n**Given** the CI pipeline runs,\n**When** the scope-guardrail suite executes against the OpenAPI spec and the route registry,\n**Then** it passes only when zero endpoints match an excluded category (mobile, attachments, real-time), and it runs on every CI build.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05 (criterion 1); NFR-010; BR-014; BR-015.\n- **Verification.** `test-guardrail-suite-zero-matches` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The patterns are `GAP-BE-764`. The registry source is `GAP-BE-770`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-013 A matching endpoint blocks the merge\n**Given** a developer adds an endpoint matching an excluded pattern (e.g. `/attachments` or `/ws`),\n**When** CI runs on the pull request,\n**Then** the guardrail test fails and blocks the merge.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05 (criterion 2).\n- **Verification.** `test-guardrail-blocks-merge-on-excluded-endpoint` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The merge-blocking mechanism is `GAP-BE-766`.\n\n### Coverage of owned stories\n\n| Story | Covered by |\n|---|---|\n| US-005-01 | FR-001, FR-002, FR-003 |\n| US-005-02 | FR-004, FR-005, FR-006 |\n| US-005-03 | FR-007, FR-008, FR-009 |\n| US-005-04 | FR-005, FR-006, FR-010, FR-011 |\n| US-005-05 | FR-012, FR-013 |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\nNo HTTP operation is defined.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-262`. The route registry contains no route in the attachment category and no real-time channel. [BR-014, BR-015, NFR-010]\n- `INV-BE-263`. Every out-of-scope call that is refused returns a structured error with code `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a message referencing the scope exclusion, never a silent failure or an empty body. [BR-016; US-005-04; NFR-011] This does not cover a refused WebSocket or SSE attempt, whose body is `GAP-BE-765`.\n- `INV-BE-264`. A refused out-of-scope request persists no data, and in particular no file content. [US-005-02]\n- `INV-BE-265`. No response to any request performs a protocol upgrade, and no change to a note is broadcast to another client or merged. [US-005-03]\n- `INV-BE-266`. A published documentation build contains a Scope section listing mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration as excluded. A build without it fails. [US-005-01]\n- `INV-BE-267`. The guardrail suite reports PASS if and only if zero endpoints match an excluded category. [US-005-05; NFR-010]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**Operations.** The module owns none, so there is no role by operation matrix of its own.\n\n| Behaviour | Who is involved | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| Receiving the refusal | Any caller of an out-of-scope path, such as a third-party client integrator | US-005-04 |\n| Running the guardrail suite | CI pipeline, with the QA engineer responsible for the regression suite | US-005-05 |\n| Publishing the Scope section | Engineering lead maintaining notes_api | US-005-01 |\n| Approving a Scope change | Reviewer or approver, not named | US-005-01, `GAP-BE-766` |\n\n- **Ownership predicate.** None. The module owns no resource, and the refusals depend on neither a note nor a user. `CON-BE-003` and `Q-BE-003` do not affect this module.\n- **Authentication on refusals.** No input says whether an `OUT_OF_SCOPE` response needs an authenticated caller or what happens when authentication fails first (`GAP-BE-768`). `ASM-BE-302` (denied unless an input grants it) is not applied here, because applying it would turn a scope refusal into a 401 and hide the scope message that US-005-04 requires.\n- **Disclosure.** An `OUT_OF_SCOPE` message and its logs contain no credential, secret, email address or one-time code. [backend.error-model disclosure rule]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.jobs | status: DERIVED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n- **Scheduled work and sweeps.** None. No input asks for a periodic scope scan.\n- **Event-triggered work.**\n  - The guardrail suite runs on every CI build and on every pull request. [NFR-010; US-005-05]\n  - The docs build lint for the Scope section runs on every docs build. [US-005-01]\n- **Retries.** None specified. A failed run is fixed in code and re-run.\n- **Idempotency keys.** None needed. The suite and the lint only read the OpenAPI document, the route registry and the docs build, so they can repeat safely. Refusals are stateless.\n- **Retention deletion.** The NFR-019 deletions do not touch this module, because it stores nothing. [backend.data-lifecycle]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.test-plan | status: DERIVED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit** (names planned)\n\n- `test-exclusion-matcher-attachment`, `test-exclusion-matcher-realtime`, `test-exclusion-matcher-mobile`: the matcher flags each excluded pattern and no allowed route. Blocked in part by `GAP-BE-764`.\n- `test-out-of-scope-error-builder`: envelope shape, code `OUT_OF_SCOPE`, message references the scope exclusion, no address, code or secret (INV-BE-263).\n- `test-scope-section-lint`: a docs build without the Scope section fails (FR-003).\n\n**Integration** (names planned)\n\n- `test-attachment-path-out-of-scope` (FR-005), `test-multipart-upload-415-out-of-scope` and `test-multipart-upload-persists-nothing` (FR-006).\n- `test-websocket-upgrade-rejected` and `test-sse-connection-rejected` (FR-008). Status assertions wait on `GAP-BE-309`.\n- `test-concurrent-patch-no-broadcast-no-merge` (FR-009): two concurrent PATCHes to one note, no broadcast, no merge.\n- `test-mobile-specific-endpoint-out-of-scope` (FR-010), blocked in part.\n\n**Contract** (names planned)\n\n- `test-guardrail-suite-zero-matches` (FR-012): runs against the OpenAPI document and the route registry, and passes only on zero matches.\n- `test-guardrail-blocks-merge-on-excluded-endpoint` (FR-013): a fixture endpoint matching an excluded pattern makes the suite fail.\n- `test-no-attachment-routes`, `test-no-realtime-endpoints` (FR-004, FR-007).\n- `test-openapi-documents-out-of-scope-error` and `test-scope-section-lists-exclusions` (FR-011, FR-001).\n\nLatency and throughput targets for the refusals are not stated, so none are tested (`backend.nfr`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\n### Open `CON-` (defined in the project spec, not re-minted)\n\n- `CON-BE-007` (non-critical): US-005-02 requires 404 for attachment paths, while US-005-04, BR-016 and NFR-011 require a scope message rather than a bare 404. The reading used is `ASM-BE-753`.\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-753` | An attachment path returns status 404 with the standard error envelope, code `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a scope-exclusion message. | US-005-02 fixes the status at 404. US-005-04 asks for a message \"rather than a bare 404\". A 404 carrying a body satisfies both. BR-016's wording on \"404 ambiguity\" is not fully met, so `CON-BE-007` stays open. | A decision on `CON-BE-007` |\n| `ASM-BE-754` | The multipart upload refusal uses code `OUT_OF_SCOPE`, not the proposed `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE`. | `OUT_OF_SCOPE` is the only code cited in an input for this case. [US-005-04] `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE` is a proposed name (`ASM-BE-301`). | `GAP-BE-769` closing artefact |\n| `ASM-BE-755` | The Scope section is part of the published API documentation produced by `api-docs`. | US-005-01 says \"published API documentation\" and \"docs build pipeline\".
